# MGM

A referral program can encourage your existing users to tell their friends and families about your business. Using BotBonnie's member-get-member (MGM) kit, you can:

  * Reward users who successfully invite their friends to interact with your brand
  * Set the number of referrals users have to reach to get rewards
  * See the number of participants and successful referrals in the performance report



### 

How it works

Here's how an MGM campaign works:

  1. User A shares an invitation link either through LINE messages or a Facebook post.
  2. User B clicks on the invitation link shared by User A and is directed to your LINE OA or the chat page of your Facebook Page. A welcome message is shown to User B. User B can now share the campaign with friends.
  3. User A gets notified for successfully inviting User B. Different notifications can be sent based on whether User A has met the referral target.

![](https://files.readme.io/f782bb7-MGM_diagram.png)

* * *

## 

MGM kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **MGM**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f7861d4832069064f1b6acbebdb94689d8ce8076c73fdfdef5aef4afa80ec308-menu-mgm.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to enter the sub-flow of the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/0813f95-mgm-start-editing.png)

> 👍
> 
> When setting up an MGM kit for the first time, a Quick Setup will show up to guide you through the basic settings.

Click the **Invite friends** module.

![](https://files.readme.io/ccbbe73-mgm-invite-friends.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic kit settings

Under the **Basic** section, complete the following settings.

  * **Kit name** : Enter a kit name that helps you identify the kit on the console.
  * **Set campaign period** : To set a campaign period, switch on the toggle and set the start time and the end time.
  * **Out-of-period module** : Select the module you want to display if the user enters the kit outside the campaign period.

![](https://files.readme.io/6e2afc89c2e8c47d78993f2b252691f21be7a634b8e3c98dd4463968604e99b4-mgm-basic.png)

### 

3\. Set the ways to share invitations

You can let users share the referral campaign with their friends through LINE messages or Facebook posts. Use the **Share via LINE messages** and **Share via Facebook posts** toggles to enable the channels you want to use.

![](https://files.readme.io/dfcd82925abb4e7f71a0ce74065b694611b314be3ae9e55e164a6f634322bf7c-mgm-sharing-options.png)

Enter the **Button name** of the share button that will be shown in the start module for the inviter.

  * After clicking the share button for LINE, the participant can choose to share the message to friends, groups, or chats.
  * After clicking the share button for Facebook, the participant is directed to the **Share on Facebook** page on Facebook with the default invitation message.

![](https://files.readme.io/d6f375f-ShareButton_Click.png)

Under **Default message for inviting friends** or **Default post content for inviting friends** section, create the default content of the LINE message or Facebook post that will be shown after clicking the share button. Keep the default message within 78 characters to ensure readability.

The content can still be edited before being shared on Facebook or shared with a single friend, chat, or group via LINE.

![](https://files.readme.io/ee54c0f8bb1a6161250575ebf9b2dcc124caedfbc1d95b15679133d5dcae363e-mgm-sharingoptions.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> **Sharing via LINE messages** is only supported in mobile devices.
> 
>   * LINE invitation messages might not work properly if the message exceeds 1000 bytes. To avoid this issue, login to LINE Developers console, click the LINE Login channel, go to the **LIFF** tab, and enable the **shareTargetPicker** option. Alternatively, keep the message short to avoid this issue.
> 
![](https://files.readme.io/c89197a-Screen_Shot_2024-01-25_at_11.03.08_AM.png)
> 
>   * When the default invitation message for LINE includes unicode characters or emojis, the number of characters allowed is much lower than the character count shown on the console. The invitation message will be truncated if the character limit is exceeded.
> 


### 

4\. Set the referral target

If you want to reward inviters only after they have successfully referred multiple friends, enable the **Set the referral target** option and select the target number of referrals.

![](https://files.readme.io/4e91559-Referral_target.png)

When the referral target is enabled, different notifications can be sent based on whether the inviter has met the target. When disabled, the inviter receives the same message module for each successful referral.

![](https://files.readme.io/68995e7-Screen_Shot_2023-06-12_at_2.32.52_PM.png)

When **Only count referrals when the invitee is a new user** is selected, the invited user needs to be a new user who does not exist in the user list of your bot in order to count toward the referral target.

New users are defined as:

  * LINE users who are not already a follower of your LINE Official Account. Users who blocked your account and then unblocked do not count as new users.
  * Facebook users who have never messaged your Facebook Page before (via Messenger).



### 

5\. Select the welcome message for invitees

Select the start module you want to display to the invitee when they click the invitation link to join the campaign.

![](https://files.readme.io/1e048f9835ff8d1d46750a15000c5a59012c4f0f794ea41279c5f6bd0337be96-mgm-welcomemessage.png)

### 

6\. Adjust referral rankings

The **Show rankings** toggle allows you to show a ranking of participants by the number of referrals. You can change the **Button name** of the **View rankings** button shown in the start module for the inviter.

Next to **Display rank position** , select the number of ranks to display (for example, top 10 participants).

![](https://files.readme.io/bfb5b65f1d8599a3bf6c794aa34b5e5694661d7bc0b6ca8554e1dfa9bc7537b2-mgm-rankings.png)

### 

7\. Check the messaging platform status

In the **Others** section, check the status of your messaging platforms and make sure everything is set up properly.

![](https://files.readme.io/b70db3e83092cdfa3c56574d3255094e864e39f01756d00dc1c597d121a1bd11-mgm-others.png)

### 

8\. Adjust the Flows modules and add rewards

Go back to the Flows page, and adjust the content of each module to suit your campaign.

To grant rewards to participants who made successful referrals, you can add a button in the "success" notification module and set the **On click** action to an advanced kit as such a lucky wheel, scratch-off, or prize distribution kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/a8359d4-GetReward.png)

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Best practice
> 
> Test the kit before launching your campaign to ensure everything works as expected.

* * *

## 

Reset kit data

Resetting a kit is useful if you ran the campaign to do internal testing, and now want to clear all test data before officially launching the campaign.

Resetting a kit will clear all data associated with the kit.

  * All records of participant data (for example, successful invitations) will be deleted
  * The ranking status will be cleared



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> This action can't be reversed.

In the kit settings, go to the **Reset** section, then click **Reset**. You'll be prompted to type "Reset" into the input box, then click **Reset** again to confirm your choice.

![](https://files.readme.io/5f4f28eb5157824afd21041ae9bcceb38cdde1f1eee7f29e87d9eed85ce3cddf-mgm-reset.png)

* * *

## 

Performance report

To view the performance report of the MGM kit, go to **Analytics** > **Kit report** and click the view icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/7f061a3d1bfe9ae6dcbfc5d91c9c6d703a0a4351d7099a14e622abd4ed453d9f-mgm-viewperformace.png)

### 

Export the list of invited users

To export the list of invited users, click **Export list**.

![](https://files.readme.io/4972963-Screen_Shot_2023-06-12_at_1.55.15_PM.png)

Type the email address where you'd like the CSV file to be sent to, then click **Confirm**. A CSV file will be sent to you within an hour.

![](https://files.readme.io/0b7baa0-Screen_Shot_2023-06-12_at_2.02.00_PM.png)

The exported file contains a list of invited users with details such as the inviter's name, whether they are new users...etc.

![](https://files.readme.io/82d6e89-Screen_Shot_2023-06-12_at_2.05.18_PM.png)

### 

Performance summary

In the report, the following metrics are available.

  * **New users referred** : This is the number of referrals where the invited user is not already a follower of the LINE Official Account or has never had conversations with the Facebook Page.
  * **Old users reached:** This is the number of referrals where the invited user is already a follower of the LINE Official Account or has previously had conversations with the Facebook Page.
  * **Total inviters** : This is the number of users who made at least one successful referral.
  * **Average referrals per inviters** : This is the average number of referrals completed by users with at least one successful referral.

![](https://files.readme.io/e3914c3-Screen_Shot_2023-06-12_at_1.55.15_PM_copy.png)

### 

Detailed data

Select a time period to see the performance data during that period. There are four sections available:

  * Users successfully invited
  * Inviters with successful referrals
  * Distribution of successful referrals
  * Participant referral rankings

![](https://files.readme.io/d5bd988-Screen_Shot_2023-06-12_at_2.15.35_PM.png)

__Updated 5 months ago

* * *

  * [Business Hours](/docs/business-hours)



Powered by [](https://readme.com?ref_src=hub&project=botbonnie-resource-center)

# Modules

Your chatbot flow consists primarily of modules containing a series of messages. In addition, the following components can be added into your module or message that allow you to transition users through the chatbot flow or execute [triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions), such as modifying a user's parameters and tags.

  * [Buttons](doc:button): You can add up to three buttons in certain message types, such as text and carousel messages.
  * [Go-to module](doc:go-to-module): Automatically send another module after all the messages in the current module are sent.
  * [Quick replies](doc:quick-reply): Provide users with up to 13 preset conversation replies that they can click to send.
  * [User input](doc:user-input): Configure keywords that send a module or execute a triggered action based on the user's input.



* * *

## 

Modules

### 

Add a module

  1. Go to **Flows**.
  2. From the flows page, click **Add Module**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5d1ee93-add-module.png)

Once the module is added into your flow, you can begin adding messages that will be sent to your audience.

### 

Duplicate a module

  1. Select the module you'd like to duplicate.
  2. Right-click the module or click the three dots on the top-right corner of the module, then click **Copy** or click **Copy** in the top toolbar.

![](https://files.readme.io/37ac141-copy-module.png)

  3. In the flow, right-click on the place where you'd like to paste the module, then click **Paste**.

![](https://files.readme.io/d8f023e-paste-module.png)

Alternatively, you can click **Paste** in the top toolbar to add the new module to the flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/26b7834-paste-module-toolbar.png)

### 

Reposition modules

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Repositioning modules only changes their arrangement in the flows page—the order of modules sent in the chatbot are unchanged and paths won't be altered.

  * To move modules in your flow, click on the module and drag it to the desired position.
  * To select multiple modules, create a selection area by holding `SHIFT` while clicking and dragging. All modules contained in the selection area can then be dragged as a group to the desired position.

![To create a selection area, hold `SHIFT` while clicking and dragging.](https://files.readme.io/a300e8d-selecting-multiple-modules.png)

To create a selection area, hold `SHIFT` while clicking and dragging.

### 

Delete a module

To delete a module, click the three dots on the top-right corner of the module and click **Move to trash**.

![](https://files.readme.io/8512deb-delete-module.png)

Modules can be recovered from the trash can within three months, after which they are permanently deleted.

### 

Quick replies, go-to modules, and user input

A module can also contain quick replies and go-to modules which give you more flexibility in your chatbot flow:

  * [Quick replies](doc:quick-reply): Provide up to 13 preset replies that users can click on.
  * [Go-to modules](doc:go-to-module): Automatically send another module after sending the initial module.
  * [User input](doc:user-input): Send a module and execute triggered actions based on the user's input.



* * *

## 

Messages

Each module can contain five messages. Chatbot flows support several message types, such as text messages, image messages, and carousel messages, allowing you to select the [message type](doc:flows-message-types) that best suits your purpose and audience.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Add buttons
> 
> You can add buttons to your messages to allow users to complete various actions. To learn more, see [Buttons](doc:button).

### 

Add a message

To add a message to a module, click the module to open the edit screen, then under **Add a Message** , select the message type you'd like to add. A single module can contain up to five messages.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> To send more than five message at once, use a [go-to module](doc:go-to-module).

![](https://files.readme.io/b9bc85c-add-a-message.png)

### 

Duplicate message

To duplicate a message, click the copy icon on the right side of the module settings window.

![](https://files.readme.io/01d3ef3-copy-message.png)

### 

Delete a message

To delete a message, click the delete icon on the right side of the module settings window.

![](https://files.readme.io/4ff99fd-delete-message.png)

### 

Personalize messages

To personalize a message by including the user's name, click **{...}** located in the bottom right corner of the input field—this will insert the `Full Name` variable into the text input box

The `Full Name` variable is dynamically updated with the name of the user who's viewing the messages as supplied by the platform being used (e.g. Facebook, Instagram, LINE).

![](https://files.readme.io/22c1d1c-message-variable.png)

### 

Reorder messages

To reorder a message, click the up and down arrows on the right side of the module settings window.

  * Up arrow: Move the current message to an earlier position in the message sequence.
  * Down arrow: Move the current message to a later position in the message sequence.

![](https://files.readme.io/c526104-reorder-message.png)

__Updated 5 months ago

* * *

# Persona

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
>   * Facebook
>   * LINE
> 


The persona kit allows you to create engaging roles with unique names and avatars, enabling users to interact with characters that represent your brand or campaign for a more immersive experience. This feature enhances interactions by making conversations feel more dynamic and personalized.

![](https://files.readme.io/bd5db02561d75c972849ae902290689b3a8eed37804f564a4e4d9365b6e2dec6-conversation-with-persona-kit.png)

### 

Example use cases

Here are some ways you can use the persona kit:

  * **Create interactive personas for customer engagement** : Design a virtual assistant with multiple expert roles or a customer support chatbot with different personas to handle inquiries. You can also use brand characters to enhance interactive campaigns and deepen user engagement.
  * **Enhance brand storytelling in campaigns** : Use different personas to represent various aspects of your campaign, switching them dynamically based on campaign needs or user interactions.



* * *

## 

Persona kit set up

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , click on **Advanced kits** , then select **Persona** to add kit to your flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/d11a91408281b5fe347a842ee05d018bbc741dfe43c7f510d5ae73602b074e80-persona-kit.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the module to open the Persona kit sub-flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/2de4180cc41ad5b825f504559b5b1d2e47fb8c5fc854e44ad98cefe4f2102b8f-start-editing-persona.png)

### 

2\. Create a persona

Click **Settings** and select **\+ Add persona** to enter a name and upload an avatar image for the new persona. You can add up to 10 roles.

![](https://files.readme.io/98f697980836238ae4b6d957203400f17a40f416f64c2edcdc9a3fb098a84877-settings-add-persona.png)

### 

3\. Assign a persona to a module

In the persona kit sub-flow, click **\+ Add module** to create a new interaction. Then, in the module settings:

  1. [Add a message](doc:modules-and-messages#messages) to create the module's content.
  2. Switch to the **Persona** tab. Select and assign a role from the dropdown to this module. This ensures that the selected persona is displayed as the character for this interaction.

![](https://files.readme.io/19fe6ce5c98be812c7cf3fe40ab76c143d14157c0fef0598b0689eb35f0df523-select-persona.png)

# Point Card

Use BotBonnie's point card kit to build customer loyalty by letting users earn points when they shop or interact with your brand. You can set up different rewards that can be redeemed with points to motivate users.

Using the point card kit, you can easily:

  * Customize the appearance of the point card
  * Let users earn points through QR code/URL or as an incentive in other advanced kits
  * Set up different rewards that can be redeemed with points

![](https://files.readme.io/1abf56e-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_2.24.07_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Point card kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

In the **Flows** page, click **Advanced kits** , and select **Point card** to begin editing kit settings.

![](https://files.readme.io/0076b87-nav-point-card-kit.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to open the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/a703056-Screen_Shot_2023-10-23_at_11.50.41_AM.png)

Click the **Point card** module.

![](https://files.readme.io/5376a9a-point-card-module.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic kit settings

Under the Basic section, complete the following settings.

  * **Kit name** : Enter a kit name that helps you identify the kit on the console.
  * **Total points per card** : This is the maximum number of points that can be accumulated on each point card.
  * **Welcome points** : This is the number of points granted to users when they join the campaign. When points are given at the beginning, users are more motivated to continue earning points. For example, you can let users start off with 3 points already on the point card.

![](https://files.readme.io/0e498f3-Screen_Shot_2023-10-24_at_10.34.35_AM.png)

### 

3\. Set the schedule

Under the Schedule section, set up the following time periods.

  * **Campaign period** : Set the campaign period of this point card. Point collection, point redemption, and point expiration period all need to be within the campaign period.
  * **Point collection period** : Users can only earn points during this period.
    * The point collection period needs to be within the campaign period.
    * The start date of the point collection period must be earlier than the end date of the point redemption period
  * **Point redemption period** : Users can only exchange points for rewards during this period.
    * The point redemption period needs to be within the campaign period.
    * The point redemption period is different from the reward redemption period under the Rewards section. Users need to redeem points for rewards during the point redemption period. However, if the reward needs to be further exchanged for or used (e.g. users need to visit a physical store to retrieve it), the period will be based on the reward redemption period.
  * **Point expiration period** : All points that are not redeemed yet will expire after this time period.
    * We recommend not changing this setting once you have started issuing points to avoid disputes.
    * If the point expiration setting is later than the end date of the campaign period, points will expire on the campaign end date. Here's an example. Let's say the **Amount of time after last earned point** is set to one month. If the user earned points on Oct 10, the points will expire after one month on Nov 10. If the user earned some more points on Oct 25, the expiration date of all points will extend to Nov 25, including the points earned on Oct 10. However, all points will expire by the end of the campaign period.

![](https://files.readme.io/2df2333-Screen_Shot_2023-10-24_at_10.30.31_AM.png)

### 

4\. Customize the appearance

Under the Appearance section, you can select an existing **Theme**. Next to **Preview** , you can use the drop-down menu on the right to preview what each page looks like.

You can also enable **Customize appearance** to make further customization on the visual appearance.

![](https://files.readme.io/996b9b7-Screen_Shot_2023-10-23_at_6.07.58_PM.png)

You can select a **Theme color** and upload your own images for the **Background** , **Earned stamp** , and **Redeemed stamp** of the point card. Refer to the image specifications shown on the console to prepare the images.

### 

5\. Set up ways to earn points

There are three ways users can earn points for point cards.

  * Use a QR code or URL
  * Advanced kit: Earn points from a triggered action
  * Advanced kit: Earn points as a prize



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Currently, only QR codes or URLs are shown in the **Ways to earn points** list.
>   * Earning points using QR codes or URLs is only supported for Facebook Messenger and LINE Official Account.
> 


![](https://files.readme.io/481e784-Screen_Shot_2023-10-24_at_10.09.39_AM.png)

#### 

Use a QR code or URL

You can let users earn points by scanning a QR code or by visiting a URL. Up to 20 can be created.

To create one, click **Add QR code or URL**.

![](https://files.readme.io/9dd41e8-Screen_Shot_2023-10-23_at_6.49.18_PM.png)

  * **Issue points** : This is the number of points users can receive each time they use this QR code or URL.
  * **Bot reply** : Under **When points earned successfully** and **When failed to earn points** , select a module to inform users whether they have successfully earned points from this QR code or URL. Note that sending notifications on LINE will incur extra charges.
  * **Select channel** : Select the Facebook Messenger or LINE Official Account to open when this QR code or URL is scanned.
  * **Frequency cap** : There are two options.
    * **Each user can only earn points once with this URL** : Limit each user to only earn points once with this URL or QR code.
    * **Set frequency cap** : Let users earn points up to once during the time duration you set. For example, if the frequency cap is set to 1 day and a user earned points at 18:00 today, this user will be able to use this URL to earn points again after 18:00 tomorrow.
  * **Triggered actions** : Set up any actions you want to trigger when the QR code or URL is used.



After the QR code / URL is created, click the share icon to find the QR code or URL.

![](https://files.readme.io/3011cee-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_11.43.09_AM.png)

#### 

Advanced kits: Earn points from a triggered action

In the following advanced kits, you can let users win points as a triggered action when they meet the requirements.

  * Member-get-member (MGM)
  * Serial number verification
  * Receipt registration



To set point card as a triggered action, follow the steps below for each kit:

  * **MGM** : Open the kit, find the **Meet target or not** conditions, find the "Met target" scenario, and set the **Triggered actions** to **Point Card**. Next, select a point card kit and set the number of points to issue to users.

![](https://files.readme.io/33cc215-Screen_Shot_2023-10-26_at_10.16.10_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/dfcda52-Screen_Shot_2023-10-26_at_10.19.07_AM.png)

  * **Serial number verification** : Go to the kit settings page, find the "The user entered a valid and unused serial number" scenario, and set the **Triggered actions** of the scenario to **Point Card**. Next, select a point card kit and set the number of points to issue to users.

![](https://files.readme.io/4cc8840-Screen_Shot_2023-10-26_at_9.38.29_AM.png)

  * **Receipt registration** : Go to the kit flow, find the **Receipt conditions** conditions, find the scenario for qualified receipts, and set the **Triggered actions** to **Point Card**. Next, select a point card kit and set the number of points to issue to users.

![](https://files.readme.io/6051d4b-Screen_Shot_2023-10-26_at_11.13.15_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/d59bc70-Screen_Shot_2023-10-26_at_11.20.58_AM.png)

#### 

Advanced kits: Earn points as a prize

In the following advanced kits, you can let users win points as a prize.

  * Lucky wheel
  * Daily check-in
  * Prize distribution
  * Scratch-off



To set this up, go to the settings of the kit (e.g. lucky wheel), set the prize format to **Point card** , and select a point card kit. Under **Issue points** , set the number of points to issue to users if they win this prize.

![](https://files.readme.io/50d84ad-Screen_Shot_2023-10-23_at_9.56.40_PM.png)

### 

6\. Set up rewards

Under Rewards, set up the rewards that can be redeemed with points.

![](https://files.readme.io/2502a64-Screen_Shot_2023-10-24_at_11.45.07_AM.png)

For each reward, configure the following settings. To add more rewards, click **Add reward**.

  * **Name** : Give a name to the reward. This name will be displayed to users when they get the reward.
  * **Points required** : This is the number of points required to get this reward.
  * **Format** : Select a reward format. The following formats are supported.

![](https://files.readme.io/914d7d4-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_1.34.06_PM.png)

Prize type| Description  
---|---  
**Inform users of rewards earned**|  Users are immediately presented with the reward. This reward type is ideal for digital rewards that don't require a physical exchange to occur. For example, this prize type can be used for multi-use coupon codes and digital assets such as images.  
**Physical Exchange**|  This reward type is suitable for brick-and-mortar locations and physical events, where winning users can physically redeem a reward by presenting the winning screen to an employee.This option is also suitable if you want to ship the physical reward to the users. To let users enter shipping information, select **Show the form on the page** and the following fields will be added to the reward page: **Name** , **Phone** , **Email** , **Address**.  
**Unique serial number: Text**|  This option allows you to upload a CSV file containing a list of unique serial numbers. Users who win this reward will be assigned a unique serial number based on the order of the serial number in the file until the serial number runs out. For example, the first winning user will receive serial number A001, the second user will receive A002, etc.Serial number file requirements:  
• Only CSV files are supported  
• The file must be less than 10 MB  
• The file must contain a single column with the list of unique serial numbers\ ![](https://files.readme.io/8b60599-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_2.03.18_PM.png)This reward type is suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.  
**Unique serial number: Barcode**|  This reward type is similar to the text-format unique serial number, except that the serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Select the appropriate barcode type such as Code-39.This reward type is suitable for physical stores with POS machines.  
**Unique serial number: URL**|  The CSV file should include the URL of each serial number. When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward.This reward type is suitable for giving out LINE points.  
  
  * **Thumbnail** : Upload a thumbnail image if needed. This is the thumbnail of the reward in the reward list.

  * **Image** : Upload the main reward image if needed. This is the reward image shown on the reward details page.

  * **Details** : Enter descriptions about the reward.

  * **Shipping information form** : This setting is only available for **Physical exchange**. If you want users to enter their shipping address, select **Show the form on the page**.

  * **Quantity** : Set the number of rewards available. This setting is unavailable for **Unique serial number** since the number of rewards available will be based on the number of serial numbers in the CSV file.

  * **Triggered action** : Set up any actions you want to trigger when the reward is redeemed. For example, you can add a tag to users who redeemed this reward.

  * **Reward redemption period** : Users can redeem the reward during this time period. If you set a reward redemption period beyond the campaign period, the reward can still be redeemed based on the reward redemption period even after the campaign period has ended.

  * **Send a message through chat** : Select this option if you want to send a message to the user through chat when they redeem this reward. You can edit the content of the message in the flows.




### 

7\. Check other settings

Under normal circumstances, no action is needed in this section. BotBonnie will automatically detect if your channels are successfully connected. If you run into issues here, contact Appier Support or your customer success manager for assistance.

![](https://files.readme.io/0b48737-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_10.19.02_AM.png)

### 

8\. Adjust the Flows modules

Go back to the Flows page, and adjust the content of the modules in the sub-flow to suit your campaign.

![](https://files.readme.io/30fa8df-Screen_Shot_2023-10-24_at_10.54.50_AM.png)

* * *

## 

Reset kit data

Resetting a kit is useful if you ran the campaign to do internal testing, and now want to clear all test data before officially launching the campaign.

Resetting a kit will clear all data associated with the kit.

  * All points will be deleted
  * All rewards will be returned to the reward pool



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> This action can't be reversed.

In the kit settings, go to the **Reset** section, then click **Reset**. You'll be prompted to type "Reset" into the input box, then click **Reset** again to confirm your choice.

![](https://files.readme.io/73fab87-Screen_Shot_2023-10-24_at_10.55.40_AM.png)

* * *

## 

Performance reports

To view the performance reports, in the sub-flow of the point card kit, click **Kit reports**.

![](https://files.readme.io/2ca99d3-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_8.32.11_AM.png)

Type the email address where you'd like the reports to be sent to, then click **Confirm**. The reports will be sent to you shortly.

![](https://files.readme.io/e15b35a-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_8.40.47_AM.png)

You will find three CSV files in the email.

  * **Loyalty Point Card Distribution Report** : This report shows the welcome points issued to users initially and the points issued for each point source.
    * **Distributed points** : The number of points issued each time a user earns points from this point source.
    * **Total distributions** : The number of times points are distributed from this point source.
    * **Total distributed points** : The total number of points distributed from this point source

![](https://files.readme.io/ea060cd-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_9.02.34_AM.png)

  * **Prize Inventory Status** : The report shows the points required to redeem the reward, the number of rewards redeemed by users, and the number of remaining rewards.

![](https://files.readme.io/a9bc24d-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_9.42.56_AM.png)

  * **Loyalty Point Card Prize Redemption Report** : Each row represents each time a reward is redeemed by users. The report shows the user who redeemed the reward, the platform they used, the redeemed time, the time of physical exchange, and the profile picture of the user.

![](https://files.readme.io/e044002-Screen_Shot_2023-10-27_at_9.53.40_AM.png)

# Prize Distribution Kit

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
> The prize distribution kit is available for the following channels:
> 
>   * Facebook
>   * Instagram
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
> 


The prize distribution kit allows you to give away prizes to all users who are directed to the prize distribution kit. Differing from other advanced kits where you set a winning probability to only let some users win a prize, prize distribution kit is suitable for campaigns where you want all eligible users to get a prize.

With the prize distribution kit, you can easily:

  * Hand out prizes to users
  * See the number of prizes that are distributed and redeemed
  * Export the list of winners to a CSV file
  * View performance report



### 

Example use case: Free gift for LINE followers

A brand is giving the followers of its LINE official channel a free gift for Thanksgiving. The marketers can connect the rich menu of their LINE official channel to BotBonnie's prize distribution kit. Users who click the gift button in the LINE rich menu can get the free gift.

![](https://files.readme.io/d9c705f-Screen_Shot_2023-02-22_at_4.05.14_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Prize distribution kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

In the **Flows** page, click **Advanced kits** , select **Prize distribution** , and click **Add kit** to add the kit to your flows.

![](https://files.readme.io/f4fc339-nav-prize-distribution-kit.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to enter the sub-flow of the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/1b64b81-prize-dist-start-editing.png)

Click the **Prize distribution** module.

![](https://files.readme.io/c85548b-prize-dist-module.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic kit settings

Under Basic section, enter a **Kit name** to help you identify the kit in the Flows and set a **Campaign period**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f938723-Screen_Shot_2023-02-21_at_6.18.12_PM.png)

#### 

Number of entries

You can set the maximum number of times a user can enter the campaign.

Alternatively, you can select **Use a parameter value as the number of entries** to dynamically apply different numbers to users based on the parameter value associated with each user.

If users are still eligible for more entries after a prize is distributed, there will be a **Get another one** button in the prize distributed page.

![](https://files.readme.io/2df5f5f-Screen_Shot_2023-02-17_at_6.49.54_PM.png)

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> To create a parameter, go to **Audience > User properties > Parameters** and click **New parameter**.

### 

3\. Set prize settings

Configure the following prize settings.

  * **Prize name** : Type the name of the prize (e.g. "20% off coupon"). This name will be displayed to users when they win the prize.
  * **Thumbnail** : Upload a thumbnail image of the prize if needed. This image will be shown in the prize list.
  * **Detailed image** : Upload the main prize image if needed. This image will be shown in the prize result page.
  * **Prize format** : Select a prize format. The following prize formats are supported in the prize distribution kit.



Prize type| Description  
---|---  
**Inform users of prizes won**|  Users are immediately notified of their winning status. This prize type is ideal for digital prizes that don't require a physical exchange to occur.For example, this prize type can be used for multi-use coupon codes and digital assets such as images.  
**Physical exchange**|  This prize type is suitable for brick-and-mortar locations and physical events, where winning users can physically redeem a prize by presenting the winning screen to an employee.  
**Unique serial number: Text**|  This option allows you to upload a CSV file containing a list of unique serial numbers. Users who win this prize will be assigned a unique serial number based on the order of the serial number in the file until the serial number runs out. For example, the first winning user will receive serial number A001, the second user will receive A002, etc.Serial number file requirements:  
• Only CSV files are supported  
• The file must be less than 10 MB  
• The file must contain a single column with the list of unique serial numbers\ ![](https://files.readme.io/8b60599-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_2.03.18_PM.png)This prize type is suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.  
**Unique serial number: Barcode**|  This prize type is similar to the text-format unique serial number, except that the serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Select the appropriate barcode type such as Code-39.This prize type is suitable for physical stores with POS machines.  
**Unique serial number: URL**|  The CSV file should include the URL of each serial number. When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward.This prize type is suitable for giving out LINE points. Users can redeem this type of prize with one simple click.  
  
  * **Quantity** : Set the total number of prizes that can be distributed. This setting is unavailable for **Unique serial number** since the number of prizes available will be based on the number of serial numbers in the CSV file.
  * **Redemption period** :
    * **Never expire** : Users can redeem the prize at anytime.
    * **Apply redemption period** : Set a time period for users to redeem the prize and the prize will be expired after the end time. The expiration date will be displayed to users when they win the prize.
  * **Apply daily maximum** : If you want to set a maximum number of prizes that can be distributed per day, select the option and type the maximum number of prizes that can be distributed each day.
    * Click **Divide evenly by days** to evenly divide the prize quantity by the number of days in the campaign period. If you edit the quantity or campaign period, the changes will not be reflected automatically. You will need to click this button again.
    * If the actual number of prizes distributed on a day is less than the daily maximum, the remaining prizes will be accumulated to the next day.

![](https://files.readme.io/ac82bce-prize_quantity.png)

  * **Send a message through chat** : Select this option if you want to send a message to the user who won this prize. You can edit the content of the message in the flows.



### 

4\. Customize the result messages

You can customize the message that will be shown to users in the result page when the user didn't get a prize. There are two scenarios:

  * When prizes run out
  * When daily maximum is reached

![](https://files.readme.io/a1001fc-Screen_Shot_2023-02-22_at_11.08.09_AM.png)

### 

5\. Add terms and conditions

Modify the terms and conditions of this campaign or type any information about the campaign to suit your needs. These terms and conditions are displayed at the bottom of the result pages.

![](https://files.readme.io/d07fc29-terms_and_conditions.png)

### 

6\. Other settings

Under normal circumstances, no action is needed in this section. BotBonnie will automatically detect if your channels are successfully connected with BotBonnie. If you run into issues here, contact Appier Support or your customer success manager for assistance.

![](https://files.readme.io/978da52-othersettings.png)

### 

7\. Adjust the Flows modules

Go back to the Flows page, and adjust the content of other modules in the sub-flow to suit your campaign.

![](https://files.readme.io/3b8152f-Screen_Shot_2023-02-22_at_3.01.54_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Reset kit data

Resetting a kit is useful if you ran the campaign to do internal testing, and now want to clear all test data before officially launching the campaign.

Resetting a kit will clear all data associated with the kit.

  * All records of participant data (e.g. user details, entries, and prizes) will be deleted
  * All prizes will be returned to the prize pool
  * The kit report will be cleared



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> This action can't be reversed.

In the kit settings, go to the **Reset records** section, then click **Reset**. You'll be prompted to type in `Reset` into the input box, then click **Reset** again to confirm.

![](https://files.readme.io/fb9eca5-reset.png)

* * *

## 

Performance report

To view the performance report of the prize distribution kit, go to **Analytics > Kit report** and click the view icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/97e4646-kit-report.png)

In the report, the following metrics are available.

  * **Total participants** : This is the number of users who entered the campaign, including user who didn't get a prize.
  * **Number of prizes distributed** : This is the number of prizes distributed to users.
  * **Prizes redeemed** : This is the number of prizes that are redeemed by the users.



Under Prize status, you can monitor the number of prizes left in the prize pool in the **Remaining** column.

![](https://files.readme.io/e25ebd6-Screen_Shot_2023-02-21_at_6.58.31_PM.png)

### 

Export winner list

To export the list of winners and their related data, click **Export list** in the top-right corner.

![](https://files.readme.io/6c97259-Screen_Shot_2023-02-21_at_2.47.29_PM.png)

Type the email address where you'd like the CSV file to be sent to, then click **Confirm**. A CSV file will be sent to you within an hour.

![](https://files.readme.io/c24847c-Screen_Shot_2023-02-21_at_2.48.58_PM.png)

The exported file contains a list of users who won a prize with details such as user name, prize won, drawn time...etc.

![](https://files.readme.io/a8b98a2-Screen_Shot_2023-02-21_at_2.14.36_PM.png)

__Updated 5 months ago

* * *

[Serial Number Verification](/docs/serial-number-verification-kit)[Point Card](/docs/point-card-kit)



---

# Quick Reply

## 

Overview

Quick replies are preset replies that a user can click on to respond to a message. A module can contain up to 13 quick replies.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> All quick replies options will disappear from the chat if:
> 
>   * The user directly continues in the flow, i.e. by responding with user input, without selecting any quick reply options.
>   * The user selects a quick reply option.
> 


![](https://files.readme.io/5d5bc1e-quick-reply.png)

### 

Limitations

  * Quick reply button names are limited to 20 characters or less.
  * Quick replies aren't supported for the following messaging platforms:
    * KakaoTalk
    * WhatsApp, when used outside of a Message Template Message (MTM)



### 

Quick replies and buttons

While quick replies and [buttons](doc:button) appear similar to buttons, both of which have labels and can be clicked by users, there are several major differences between the two:

| Quick replies| Buttons  
---|---|---  
**Maximum**|  A module can contain up to 13 quick replies.| A message can contain up to 3 buttons.  
**Behavior after clicking**|  All quick replies disappear after the user clicks one.| Buttons persist in the chat after being clicked and can be clicked repeatedly.  
**Linking to a URL**|  Not supported. Quick replies can only link to other flow modules.| Supported.  
**Connecting line color in flows**|  In the flow, connections to quick replies are represented by blue lines.![](https://files.readme.io/88656e7-quick-reply-module.png)| In the flow, connections to buttons are represented by gray lines.![](https://files.readme.io/e76c242-button-module.png)  
  
* * *

## 

Adding quick replies to a module

  1. Open the module you'd like to edit and click **\+ Add Quick Reply**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f661cc8-add-quick-reply.png)

  2. Under **Button name** , input the preset reply.
     1. Note: If the quick reply will be used to send a user's location in a LINE channel (**On click** set to **Send location**), the button name will be set to "Send location" and can't be edited.

![](https://files.readme.io/89870f2-quick-reply-button-name.png)

  3. Under **On click** , select what you'd like the quick reply to do after being clicked by the user:
     1. **Postback module** : Select a module to send.
     2. **Send location** : Allow the user to send their current location. Only available for LINE.

![](https://files.readme.io/13a8d9c-quick-reply-on-click.png)

  4. (Optional) Click **Set up triggered actions** to add triggered actions. For configuration details, see [Triggered Actions](doc:triggered-actions#triggered-action-settings).

![](https://files.readme.io/e6baeac-quick-reply-triggered-actions.png)

  5. Click **Done** and deploy your changes.



## 

Deleting a quick reply

To delete a quick reply, open the quick reply's settings, then click **Delete** in the bottom left corner of the settings window.

![](https://files.readme.io/66c98ac-delete-quick-reply.png)

__Updated 11 months ago

# Quick Start Guide

This quick start guide will help you set up and start using the basics of BotBonnie in just a few steps, enabling you to unlock the full potential of automated marketing across all your channels.

  1. Connect your messaging channels
  2. Create your flow in the no-code automation builder
  3. Initiate one-way or two-way conversations
  4. Analyze insights from reports



* * *

## 

1\. Connect your messaging channels

Connect the messaging channels your brand uses.

  * [Instagram](doc:channels-instagram)
  * [KakaoTalk](doc:channels-kakaotalk)
  * [LINE](doc:channels-line)
  * [Messenger](doc:channels-messenger)
  * [WebChat](doc:channels-webchat)
  * [WhatsApp](doc:channels-whatsapp)
  * [Viber](doc:channels-viber)



* * *

## 

2\. Create your flow in the no-code automation builder

Build a chatbot flow by adding different types of conversational modules (e.g. text or image modules), add interactive elements like buttons, and advanced marketing kits like scratch-off cards and surveys.

See [Flow](doc:flows) to learn more about how to start creating your first chatbot flow.

* * *

## 

3\. Initiate one-way or two-way conversations

You can use the following BotBonnie tools to initiate conversations with your brand's customers in other ways other than your chatbot flow:

  * Initiate one-way conversations by sending a [broadcast](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts).
  * Initiate two-way conversations using [live chat](doc:live-chat) or growth tools like [Instagram auto-replies](doc:auto-reply-in-instagram).



* * *

## 

4\. Analyze insights from reports

Finally, use BotBonnie's various analytics tools and reports to better understand specific customer flows, find breakpoints in the customer journey, and optimize your flow through data-driven analysis.

  * [Dashboard](doc:dashboard)
  * [Agent Analytics](doc:analytics-agent-analytics)
  * [Flow Insight](doc:flow-insight)



__Updated 5 months ago

* * *

[Getting Started with BotBonnie](/docs/getting-started-with-botbonnie)[Getting Started with Channel Integrations](/docs/channels-getting-started)

# Random Reply

The random reply kit can be used in campaigns or games where you want to randomly show a different module to users each time. Using this kit, you can:

  * Add multiple modules and BotBonnie will randomly display one module to the user
  * You can set the probability for each reply.



### 

Example use case

Here are some examples of how the random reply kit can be used.

  * Run a fortune-telling game by having each module be a different fortune result
  * Conduct a random prize draw by setting each module to be a different prize



* * *

## 

Random Reply kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Random reply**.

![](https://files.readme.io/bbce059-nav-random-reply.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to open the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/f2f0950-random-reply-start-editing.png)

### 

2\. Add message modules

Next, click **Add module** to create some modules. From these modules, BotBonnie will randomly display one to users.

![](https://files.readme.io/a1de1e6-Screen_Shot_2023-08-18_at_7.00.25_PM.png)

### 

3\. Set the probability

Click **Settings** and set the probability for each module. You can also click **Divide evenly** to divide the probability equally among the modules.

![](https://files.readme.io/41a44bd-Screen_Shot_2023-08-18_at_7.04.00_PM.png)

### 

4\. Connect the kit to the rest of the flow

Go back to the Flows page, and connect the random reply kit to the rest of the flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/9607d47-Screen_Shot_2023-09-06_at_4.35.35_PM.png)

__Updated 5 months ago

# Receipt Registration

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
> The receipt registration kit is available for the following channels:
> 
>   * Facebook
>   * Instagram
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
> 


The receipt registration kit helps you collect and understand users' purchase behaviors. Users can upload receipt photos or enter receipt details, and you can:

  * Store and organize receipt and user data.
  * Filter receipt submissions to identify user's eligibility for rewards.



* * *

## 

Receipt registration kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Receipt registration**.

![](https://files.readme.io/7a486433945d01a140456e8bd085fc97815dfea654c65580a1d8bcc5bb63fd9a-receipt-registration-kit-new.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the module to open the kit's sub-flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/9ea752336c40fd6adc35caf49861f046ac30e7da37fe43cc8e32a5ebc88a556f-start-editing.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic kit settings

Click the **Receipt registration kit** module to set up your kit. Each module has three tabs containing configuration options:

  * **Settings**
  * **Others**
  * **Reset**



#### 

Settings

Specify the period for when users can submit receipts to manage your campaign effectively.

![](https://files.readme.io/8eeb80667f6d9aaa3d562bed7b84ea617f71583138038e23163b2c2a0fd515d1-registration-period.png)

Enter your Google account email and click **Create Sheet**. A spreadsheet link will be generated automatically to collect receipt data.

![](https://files.readme.io/dbc81464b4bcf42f709fb56aaec19a6f8ecc36b9cc95f46f1b4ec2785326c7c2-add-google-sheet.png)

#### 

Others

Check the integration settings and make sure all the IDs and domains are set up correctly across supported channels.

![](https://files.readme.io/8cc8a6f7b06c30606afed965ee7be150461dea92a5ca754f9ff6528682a62daa-others-line-liff-facebook.png)

#### 

Reset

Use the **Reset** button to clear the receipt data.

![](https://files.readme.io/e885939f517d65c755a30d030c25660334c8e9c9d8cf51ccce9f3d2b50efac72-reset-records.png)

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> Resetting the kit will permanently delete all users' receipt data. This action cannot be undone.

### 

3\. Set the message for upload receipt

Navigate to the **Upload receipt** module to customize the **Messages** and **User input**.

#### 

Messages

By default, the bot message includes an image, text, and two buttons, which are **Upload receipt photo** and **Enter receipt info**. You can modify these messages to match your brand's style and tone.

![](https://files.readme.io/78fd1fb905302f6285870bd6203ad6029986146170ab5071ede3441cc456c342-upload-receipt.png)

#### 

User input

**Behavior #1** is the default for the "Upload receipt" module. Configure the bot's response and triggered actions for this scenario.

Alternatively, use the **\+ Add Behavior** button to create custom input scenarios. This lets you specify the input method, bot replies, and triggered actions.

![](https://files.readme.io/ca36738092bfe266c7d5113e83dc82cff09337d746a40288cfbb6759ccad4545-upload-receipt-user-input.png)

### 

4\. Customize predefined scenarios

Customize the predefined scenarios for receipt recognition to meet your requirements. Each scenario comes with default text and settings you can customize to match your brand.

There are seven predefined scenarios available for customization:

Predefined scenarios| Description  
---|---  
Qualified| Sent when a receipt meets all validation criteria to confirm successful submission and encourage user participation.  
Not qualified| Sent when a receipt fails validation criteria to provide rejection reasons and instructions for correction and resubmission.  
Duplicate| Sent when the same receipt is submitted multiple times to inform users that it cannot be processed again.  
Campaign has ended| Sent when a submission is made after the campaign ends to notify users and direct them to future opportunities.  
Taiwan Ministry of Finance (MOF) system is busy| Sent when the Taiwan MOF system is busy to reassure users about retry attempts and encourage patience.  
Taiwan Ministry of Finance (MOF) database not updated| Sent when the Taiwan MOF database has not been updated to provide status updates and explain potential processing delays.  
Recognition failure| Sent when the system can't recognize a receipt, such as when users upload a low-quality image or unsupported file type, to guide users in resubmitting a clearer image.  
  
Navigate to the **Receipt conditions** module to customize entry rules, bot replies, and triggered actions for each predefined scenarios.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Rules are evaluated in the order listed in the **Receipt conditions** module. For example, placing disqualifying conditions before eligibility checks ensures that the bot processes them correctly.
> 
> ![](https://files.readme.io/1fbb67106dab356578febf5aa4f4bed43eaa6685a4a6ef5bd7a6cacd6f3d3947-arrange-order.png)
> 
> In this case, when you put "successfully qualified" as **Scenario #1** and "receipt data duplicate" as **Scenario #2** , the bot will first check whether the uploaded receipt meets **Scenario #1** before verifying if it is a duplicate.

* * *

## 

Check the user's receipt data

After starting the campaign, you can follow the steps below to view receipt data submitted by your users:

  1. Go to the **Receipt registration** module.
  2. Open the **Settings** tab and click **Open spreadsheet**. You need to log in to your Google account to see the data collected.

![](https://files.readme.io/c58ec642c4fe092d4d1e7d12326a96a8e35de5546ce1c30eaa6aca0c7dc8e792-open-spreadsheet.png)

__Updated 5 months ago

* * *

# Condition

The condition kit is designed to enhance user engagement and interaction through personalized experiences. Using the condition kit, you can specify different responses based on a user's tags or parameters, making interactions more dynamic and tailored. Condition kits can be used for:

  * **Channel-specific welcome messages** : Send different welcome messages depending on which channel the user is on.
  * **Action-based rewards** : For example, you can create a kit that gives a reward to users who have successfully linked their social account or completed a chatbot survey for the first time.
  * **Personalized marketing campaigns and recommendations** : Tailor messages or deliver specific product recommendations based on user behavior and interests. Offer exclusive discounts to frequent buyers or notify browsers about ongoing sales, ensuring targeted and relevant communication.



* * *

## 

Using condition kits

In the following guide, we'll create a condition kit that rewards new customers who have completed a survey with a prize code.

### 

1\. Add the condition kit

To add a condition kit to your flow, click **Rule kits** , then select **Conditions**.

![](https://files.readme.io/25ccf53-add-condition-kit.png)

### 

2\. Add condition-based scenarios

Click the conditions module you just added, then click **+Scenario**.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note: Multiple scenarios
> 
> The order in which the scenarios are created is also the priority they're evaluated in. If the conditions of multiple scenarios are met at the same time, the scenario that was created first will be used.

![](https://files.readme.io/63cf1ba-add-scenario.png)

Under the new scenario, go to **Rules** , then click on the condition to configure its settings. For example, you can add a condition that specifies users with the "Survey completed" tag should be sent the module containing the prize code.

![](https://files.readme.io/0bfd5a2-add-condition.png)

#### 

Add multiple conditions

To add additional conditions, click **Add condition**. You can specify up to 5 conditions per scenario. Note that the scenario will only be used if all the conditions are met.

### 

3\. Add a default scenario

The default scenario specifies which module should be sent if the user doesn't satisfy the conditions for any other scenario.

In this example, the default scenario consists of all users who haven't completed the survey, i.e. users missing the "Survey completed" tag will receive a message prompting them to take the survey.

![](https://files.readme.io/6ddae67-default-scenario.png)

# Scratch-Off

Use the scratch-off kit to create a campaign that allows users to play a scratchcard game from their mobile device or web browser. With the scratch-off kit, you can easily:

  * Update prize inventory
  * Export the list of participant to a CSV file
  * View detailed performance data in the kit report page



* * *

## 

Scratch-off kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow**. Click **Advanced kits** , select **Scratch-off** , then click **Add kit** to add the kit to your flow. To start editing the kit's settings, click **Start editing** or double-click the kit module.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/ccbadb2-nav-scratch-off.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic scratch-off kit settings

Enter a **Kit name** to help you identify the kit in your flow, and set a **Campaign period** by selecting a start and end time.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/7933fc9-scratch-off-name-campaign-period.png)

#### 

Number of entries

This is the maximum number of times a user can play the scratch-off game.

  * **Maximum entries per user** : Specify the total number of time users can play the scratch-off game.
  * **Maximum entries per day** : Specify the number of time users can play the scratch-off game each day. The count resets at midnight each day.
  * **Use a parameter value as the number of entries per person** : Dynamically apply different numbers of entries to users based on the parameter value associated with each user.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/b7e9999-scratch-off-number-of-entries.png)

#### 

Example: Use a parameter value as the number of entries per person

In the following example, we want to encourage users to participate in a free trial of a new product in your physical stores by rewarding participants with a chance to play the scratch-off game. To accomplish this, we'll use a scratch-off kit using a parameter value as the number of entries per person.

  1. Set up the in-store scannable QR code. Go to **Growth tools > Start chat with QR code** and select the module containing the promotional message.
  2. Give users an additional chance to play the scratch-off game after scanning the QR code. Set **Triggered actions > Calculate parameter value** to add `1` to a parameter (e.g. `scratch_off_attempts`) when the user scans the QR code.



After completing these steps, we can let users scan the QR code to get another chance to play the scratch-off game after participating in the in-store free trial.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/c72ecb3-Screen_Shot_2023-11-07_at_3.50.30_PM.png)

### 

3\. Upload scratch-off card image

Upload the image for the scratch card. Images must meet the following requirements:

  * The file format must be JPG or PNG
  * The aspect ratio should be 1:1 (square)



### 

4\. Add terms and conditions

The kit provides a default terms and conditions statement enumerating the rules for participation in this promotion. These terms and conditions are displayed at the bottom of the scratch card screen. Modify this field if you'd like to customize the terms and conditions statement to suit your campaign.

![](https://files.readme.io/d38fcbf-scratch-off-terms-and-conditions.png)

### 

5\. Add prizes and set prize settings

  * To add a prize, click **Add prize**
  * To remove a prize, click **X** in the top right-hand corner of the prize

![](https://files.readme.io/f515a29-scratch-off-add-remove-prizes.png)

  * **Prize name** : The name of the prize that's displayed to the user when they win.

![](https://files.readme.io/9219ffe-prize-name-example.png)

  * **Prize format** : Prizes can have one of the following formats:



Prize format| Description  
---|---  
**Inform users of prizes won**|  Users are immediately notified of their winning status. This prize type is ideal for digital prizes that don't require a physical exchange to occur.  
  
For example, this prize type can be used for digital assets such as images.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/0894be5-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_11.52.57_AM.png)  
**Physical exchange**|  This prize type is suitable for brick-and-mortar locations and physical events, where winning users can physically redeem a prize by presenting the winning screen to an employee.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/a1d9b32-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_12.02.12_PM.png)  
**Unique serial number**|  Upload a CSV file containing a list of unique serial numbers (text, barcode, or URL) that users can exchange for a prize. If this prize type is selected, winners will see the serial number when they win. For example:  
  
• **Text** : Users who win this prize will be assigned a unique serial number from the CSV file in sequential order. Serial number are suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.  
• **Barcode** : The serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Barcodes are suitable for physical stores with POS machines.  
• **URL** : The CSV file should contain a list of URLs. When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward. URLs are suitable for distributing LINE points, as it allows users to redeem points with a single click.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/08193c1-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_1.59.22_PM.png)  
  
Please note the following serial number file requirements:  
• Only CSV files are supported  
• The file must be less than 10 MB  
• The file must contain a single column with the list of serial numbers  
![](https://files.readme.io/8b60599-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_2.03.18_PM.png)  
**Fixed serial number**|  Specify a serial number (text, barcode, or URL) that users can exchange for a prize. If this prize type is selected, winners will see the serial number when they win. For example:  
  
• **Text** : Users who win this prize will be shown the serial number. Serial number are suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.  
• **Barcode** : The serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Barcodes are suitable for physical stores with POS machines.  
• **URL** : When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward. URLs are suitable for distributing LINE points, as it allows users to redeem points with a single click.  
**Point card**|  Select an existing point card kit to send to users who win.  
  
  * **Quantity** : Set the number of prizes to distribute. This setting is unavailable for prizes with the prize type set to **Unique serial number**.
  * **Probability** : Set the probability of winning this prize.



#### 

Daily maximum

Set an upper limit for the number of prizes that can be distributed per day.

To distribute approximately the same number of prizes each day for the duration of the campaign, check **Divide evenly by days**. For example, if the quantity of "Prize A" is 10 and the campaign is five days, checking this option will distribute "Prize A" approximately twice per day.

When this option is checked, the estimated maximum number of prizes to be distributed per day will be shown:

![ ](https://files.readme.io/bfa8e85-scratch-off-daily-maximum.png)

### 

6\. Set a module for losing participants

Under **When no prize is won** , select the module or message that users should see if they don't win a prize.

![](https://files.readme.io/9fe8e36-scratch-off-losing-message.png)

* * *

## 

Scratch-off kit report

To view the scratch-off kit report, in the left-hand navigation bar, go to **Analytics > Kit Report**, then open the report you'd like to view. Scratch-off kit reports contain a summary of the number of participants and prizes distributed.

![ ](https://files.readme.io/6ff2296-scratch-off-kit-report.png)

In addition, under **Detailed data** , graphs are provided to visualize the kit's performance data for a specific time period. You can view:

  * The total number of participants
  * The total number of prizes won, broken down by prize (counts for total prizes and counts for individual prizes)
  * The total number of draws (scratch-off attempts)

![](https://files.readme.io/235b512-scratch-off-kit-report-detailed-data.png)

### 

Export the list of participants

From the kit report page, you can export a CSV containing a list of participants and their associated data.

  1. Click **Export participant list**.
  2. In the modal that pops up, input the email address where you'd like the CSV file to be sent to.
  3. Click **Confirm**. An email containing the file will be sent to the email address you specified within one hour.



The participant list file contains the following details:

  * User ID
  * User Name
  * Conversation Platform
  * User Gender
  * Prize Title
  * Prize
  * Serial number
  * Kit ID
  * Drawn time
  * Redeemed time
  * User profile

![](https://files.readme.io/c379236-scratch-off-kit-download-report.png)

* * *

## 

Reset a kit

Reset a kit to refresh all data associated with the kit:

  * All records of participant data (e.g. user details, draws, and wins) will be deleted
  * All prizes will be returned to the prize pool
  * All data in the kit report will be cleared



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> Resetting the kit can't be reversed. Once the kit is refreshed, all records of participant data and prize distribution (including the kit report) will be cleared and all prizes will be returned to the prize pool.

In the scratch-off kit settings, go to the **Reset** section, then click **Reset**. You'll be prompted to type in "Reset" into the input box, then click **Reset** again to confirm your choice.

![](https://files.readme.io/c7fb23a-kit-reset.png)

* * *

## 

Prize distribution probability

When setting distribution probabilities for prizes, please note the following behaviors:

  * The probability of losing
  * The probability when a prize runs out
  * Calculating the maximum daily prize distribution



### 

Probability of losing

If the probabilities don't add up to 100%, the remainder will be the probability of losing (not winning any prize).

For example, given two prizes, "Prize A" and "Prize B", each with a probability of 30%, totaling 60% (30% + 30% = 60%), the probability of losing would be 40% (100% - 60% = 40%).

### 

When a prize runs out

If there are multiple prizes and one prize runs out, the probability of winning the remaining prize(s) stays the same, and the probability of not winning a prize increases.

Prize| Original probability| Probability after Prize A runs out  
---|---|---  
Prize A| 10%| 0% (Runs out)  
Prize B| 20%| 20% (Unchanged)  
Prize C| 30%| 30% (Unchanged)  
No prize| 40%| 50%  
  
### 

Maximum daily prize distribution

The daily prize distribution maximum is calculated in real-time for each prize based on how many days have passed since the beginning of the campaign. The calculation for the maximum number of prizes to be distributed by the end of a given day is calculated as follows:

  * `Campaign elapsed time (days) / Total campaign duration (days) * Prize pool quantity`



The difference between the total expected number of distributed prizes (the total number of prizes that should be distributed by this point in the campaign) and the actual number of distributed prizes (the total number of prizes that have already been distributed so far during the campaign) determines the distribution limit for that day.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If the daily distribution maximum isn't a whole number, it will be rounded up to the nearest whole number. This can result in variations in the daily prize distribution limit for each day of the campaign.

For example, given a campaign period of seven days, and a single prize, "Prize A", with a quantity of 30 and a 100% probably of winning (no other prizes, no chance of losing), the estimated counts for the daily distribution are listed in the table below:

Day| Daily calculation| Prizes distributed so far| Prizes to distribute today  
---|---|---|---  
1| `(1/7 * 30) = 4.28 → 5`  
  
A maximum of 5 prizes should be distributed by the end of day one.| 0 prizes| `(5 - 0) = 5`  
  
Up to 5 prizes can be distributed today.  
2| `(2/7 * 30) = 8.57 → 9`  
  
A maximum of 9 prizes should be distributed by the end of day two.| 5 prizes| `(9 - 5) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
3| `(3/7 * 30) = 12.85 → 13`  
  
A maximum of 13 prizes should be distributed by the end of day three.| 9 prizes| `(13 - 9) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today  
4| `(4/7 * 30) = 17.14 → 18`  
  
A maximum of 18 prizes should be distributed by the end of day four.| 13 prizes| `(18 - 5) = 5`  
  
Up to 5 prizes can be distributed today.  
5| `(5/7 * 30) = 21.42 → 22`  
  
A maximum of 22 prizes should be distributed by the end of day five.| 18 prizes| `(22 - 18) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
6| `(6/7 * 30) = 25.71 → 26`  
  
A maximum of 26 prizes should be distributed by the end of day six.| 22 prizes| `(26 - 22) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
7| `(7/7 * 30) = 30`  
  
A maximum of 30 prizes should be distributed by the end of day seven.| 26 prizes| `(30 - 26) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
  
__Updated 5 months ago

* * *

# Segments

BotBonnie segments allow you to segment users by specifying conditions, such as users who have a certain tag or interacted with your bot in a certain time frame. After creating a segment, you can:

  * Download a CSV file containing details about users in the segment, e.g. username or first interaction time.
  * Send a [LINE narrowcast](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts#line) to users in the segment.



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> LINE narrowcast
> 
> Use [LINE narrowcasts](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts#line) to reach your audience with pinpoint accuracy, ensuring messages reach the right people at the right time to achieve higher engagement.
> 
> In addition, broadcasts support detailed reporting, including metrics like the percentage of a video watched by users, providing valuable insights for refining future campaigns.

* * *

## 

Create a segment

  1. Go to **Audience > Segments**, then click **\+ Create segment**.

![](https://files.readme.io/298b43bd81e199ce661cc84c18f2ed7dc40cadf658c7a5b71c36946b27d74e26-create-segment.png)

  2. Enter a name and add [conditions](doc:user-list#available-filter-conditions) specifying which users should be included in the segment. After adding conditions, the segment size will be displayed on the right side of the page.

![](https://files.readme.io/66b570c-segment-settings.png)

  3. Wait for the segment to finish processing. When processing is complete, the status will change from **In progress** to **Ready**. Once a segment's status is **Ready** , you'll be able to download the CSV segment file or [send LINE narrowcasts to users in this segment](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts#line).

![](https://files.readme.io/1ecc9be-segment-status.png)

* * *

## 

Manage segments

After creating the segment, you can:

  * Re-sync the segment: Recalculate the segment to ensure it's up-to-date.
  * Download the CSV segment file: Send a CSV file via email containing details about users in the segment.
  * Delete the segment.

![](https://files.readme.io/f3ccb00-manage-segments.png)

__Updated 4 months ago

# Sequence

Sequence kits use customizable delays to send sequences of messages to users, allowing you to create a more personalized and interactive chatbot. For example, while a broadcast must be scheduled at a specific time, messages in a sequence kit can be sent at different times for each user depending on the time of that user's last interaction.

* * *

## 

Using sequence kits

  1. In your flow, click **Rule kits** and choose **Sequence** to add a sequence kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/f6d1dcc-add-sequence.png)

  2. Open the sequence kit. You have the option to set up blackout window settings to prevent messages from being sent between certain times. Click **Set blackout window** to go to your bot settings page to configure blackout window settings.

![](https://files.readme.io/996b732-sequence-blackout-window.png)

  3. If you want to send a message as soon as the user reaches this point in the flow, go to the **Broadcast now** setting and select a module from the dropdown.

![](https://files.readme.io/682d422-sequence-broadcast-now.png)

  4. Next, click **\+ Sequence** to add a sequence message.



> 👍
> 
> You can add up to four sequence messages in a single kit module.

![](https://files.readme.io/373a9ab-sequence-add-new-sequence.png)

  5. Configure the following sequence message settings:
     1. **Send at** : Set the delay for the sequence message. The delay starts from when the user interacts with the message immediately prior to the sequence kit. For example, if the previous module is a text message with a button linking to the sequence kit, the delay will be calculated starting from the time the user clicks the button.
     2. **Sequence message type** : Choose a topic for the message.
     3. **Broadcast** : Select the module you'd like to send from the dropdown.

![](https://files.readme.io/f23abdc-sequence-settings.png)

__Updated 5 months ago
