# Serial Number Verification

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
> The serial number verification kit is available for the following platform:
> 
>   * Facebook
>   * Instagram
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
>   * WhatsApp
> 


The serial number verification kit allows you to control which users enter a specific flow by requesting them to provide a serial number. Using this kit, you can:

  * Upload a list of serial numbers
  * Export a report with serial number usage details
  * Reset the serial number list so that serial numbers can be reused



### 

Example use case: Prize code redemption

Our brand is running a promotion where unique prize codes are printed on bottle caps. Customers who purchase these bottles can submit prize codes via messaging channels (e.g. Messenger, LINE, WhatsApp) to redeem a free gift.

#### 

Using the serial number verification kit

The serial number verification kit allows us to create a flow that prompts users to input their contact information to receive the prize, while also ensuring that only users with valid, winning prize codes enter that flow. For example, using this kit, we can:

  1. Prompt the user to enter the prize code in a chat message
  2. If the prize code is valid, the user enters the flow to enter their contact information to receive the prize
  3. If the prize code is invalid, reply to the user notifying the user of the reason for invalidity



#### 

Add actions to flow scenarios

In addition to verifying serial numbers and distributing standard prizes, we also want to hold a raffle for all users who participated in this promotion and pick a grand winner who will win an additional special prize.

We can accomplish this by:

  1. Adding an action to the flow scenario that tags all participating users who submitted a valid serial number. For example, we can tag all participating users with the following tag: `CampaignA_20220101`.
  2. After the campaign is completed, filter the user list by the tag we applied (i.e. `CampaignA_20220101`) to retrieve the list of users who participated in this campaign.
  3. From the list of tagged users, randomly select one grand winner who will receive the special prize.



* * *

## 

Serial number verification kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Serial number verification**.

To start editing the kit's settings, click **Start editing** or double-click the kit module.

![](https://files.readme.io/8cbab85-nav-serial-number-verification-kit.png)

### 

2\. Upload a serial number file

Under the **Basic** section, upload a CSV file with a single column containing the serial numbers you'd like to use for this kit. The serial number CSV file must be under 10 MB.

![](https://files.readme.io/36a1ab4-serial-number-verification-upload-csv.png)

After you successfully upload the serial number file, the number of serial numbers contained in the file will be displayed next to **Amount**.

![](https://files.readme.io/9f2177c-serial-number-verification-amount.png)

### 

3\. Set bot replies and actions for each flow scenario

Under the **Valid serial number** and **Invalid serial number** sections, you can set bot replies and actions for the kit's four flow scenarios.

#### 

Flow scenarios

  * **Valid serial number**
    * Flow scenario #1: The user entered a valid and unused serial number
    * Flow scenario #2: The user entered a valid serial number that has already been used by that user
    * Flow scenario #3: The user entered a valid serial number that has already been used by another user
  * **Invalid serial number**
    * Flow scenario #4: The user entered an invalid serial number

![](https://files.readme.io/50db4eb-serial-number-verification-bot-reply-1.png)

#### 

Bot replies

To change the bot reply configured for a flow scenario, go to the flow scenario you want to edit the bot reply for, and from the dropdown, select the kit module you want to use as the bot reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/eff25f6-serial-number-verification-bot-reply-2.png)

#### 

Actions

Add any required actions for the flow scenarios. For an example use case of how actions can be used in your kit's flows, see Adding actions to flow scenarios.

![](https://files.readme.io/0f9d626-serial-number-verification-actions.png)

* * *

## 

Export the serial number usage report

The serial number usage report contains the usage details for each serial number. Columns corresponding to unused serial numbers will be empty. Refer to the following table for descriptions of each column in the report:

Column name| Description  
---|---  
customerId| The ID of the user.  
pageId| The ID of the page as provided by the third-party channel (e.g. LINE, Facebook).  
platform| A number representing the platform the user used to submit the serial number. For example, if this column has a value of `1`, it means the user submitted the serial number using LINE.  
  
The mappings for each platform are as follows:  
• `0`: Facebook  
• `1`: LINE  
• `2`: WebChat  
• `4`: Google Business Messages  
• `5`: Instagram  
• `6`: WhatsApp  
• `7`: AIQUA  
Used timestamp| A timestamp indicating the time that the user submitted the serial number.  
Serial Number| The serial number as specified in the uploaded CSV file.  
  
To export the serial number usage report:

  1. Go to **Basic > Upload serial number**, then click **Export report**.
  2. Input the email address where you'd like the report download link to be sent to, then click **Confirm**.
  3. When the report is ready, a download link will be sent to the email address you provided.

![](https://files.readme.io/12e3697-serial-number-verification-export-report.png)

* * *

## 

Reset serial numbers

Resetting serial numbers allows you to:

  * Make all serial numbers valid again
  * Clear all activity logs for this kit
  * Clear the serial number usage report for this kit



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> This action can't be reversed. Once the serial number list is refreshed, the kit's activity log (including all data in the serial number usage report) will be cleared and all serial numbers will become valid again.

In the serial number verification kit settings, go to the **Reset** section, then click **Reset**. You'll be prompted to type in "Reset" into the input box, then click **Reset** again to confirm your choice.

![](https://files.readme.io/dc8be31-serial-number-verification-reset.png)

__Updated about 2 months ago

# Agent Groups

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Only **Admin** and **Customer Manager** roles are permitted to create and manage agent groups. See [Team member roles and permissions](doc:settings-team-members#team-member-roles-and-permissions) to learn more about role permissions.

Agent groups allow you to automatically distribute chats using assignment rules to help balance workloads and improve team efficiency. You can also customize notification settings to ensure the right team members are notified for each chat, keeping your team communication effective.

You can create and configure multiple agent groups for different teams and purposes. Learn how agent groups work and how to:

  * Create an agent group
  * Manage your existing agent groups
  * Understand the assignment rules and notification settings



* * *

## 

How agent groups work

Agent groups connect your agents, chatbot flows, and analytics reports:

  1. Create an agent group: First, you create an agent group, add agents, and choose an assignment rule to define how chats are distributed.
  2. Connect to a workflow: Next, add a [trigger point](doc:triggered-actions#trigger-points) with the **Notify agent** action in a chatbot flow and select your group as the [notification](doc:settings-notify-agent#notification) recipient.
  3. Automate assignment: When a user triggers the **Notify agent** action, chats are automatically routed to an available agent in the group based on the rule you set.
  4. Monitor performance: Finally, an admin or customer manager can visit [**Agent analytics**](doc:analytics-agent-analytics) to track the group's performance, workloads, and use the data to optimize your support process.



* * *

## 

Create an agent group

To create an agent group, configure the group's settings and rules, then add agents to the group:

  1. Go to **Settings** > **Agent groups** , then click **\+ Create group**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e3238b80f73db68973b88560aa03a2627e56f70f18e1e4c1bb9da6ac023638be-create-agent-group.png)

  2. Enter a group name and description.
  3. In the **Assign rule** section, select your group's assignment rule.
  4. In the **Send email notifications to** section, select who receives notifications when a chat is assigned.
  5. Click **Save**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c489c356fed7bca51da94dbb355ec3c99a2620233197ebcd5ee100af800dcee4-create-group-settings.png)

* * *

## 

Manage agent groups

After creating an agent group, you can view and manage it in the agent group list (**Settings** > **Agent groups**). This page displays all your existing groups, their group managers, assignment rules, and the number of agents in each group. From here, you can:

  * Click an existing group to manage group settings and agents.
  * Click the trash bin icon to delete a group.

![](https://files.readme.io/fd0a7eb38575de71c5a716bfe01599a1142837ca4ff33d5932fc581dd7b9f82e-manage-groups.png)

### 

Manage group settings and agents

Admins and customer managers can update group settings and manage agent availability.

  * To update group settings: Click the existing group to modify the group name, description, assignment rules, or notification settings, then click **Save** to apply your changes.
  * To manage agents in an existing group:
    1. Click the existing group.
    2. In the **Agent list** section:
       * To add agents: Click **\+ Add agent** and select agents you want to add to the group.
       * To change an agent's status: In the **Available** column, select **Available** or **Away** from the dropdown menu.
       * To remove agents: Click the remove icon next to the agent you want to remove.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Notes
> 
> Availability status applies across all groups the agent belongs to. Agents set to **Away** will not receive new chats. If all agents in a group are set to **Away** , incoming chats cannot be automatically routed to that group.

![](https://files.readme.io/dfb029a16e47da38218f471cfda3e93560734668d7148c3c17747204e1680ad8-manage-agent-list.png)

### 

Delete a group

When you delete a group, the following changes occur:

  * The group will be removed from all assignment workflows and can't receive new chats.
  * Chats previously assigned through this group remain with their original agents.
  * If this group is a [Notify agent](doc:triggered-actions#notify-agent) target, you need to manually reassign it to another group or agent.
  * Performance reports for the group will be deleted, but all chat history remains available.



* * *

## 

Assignment rules and notification settings

### 

Assignment rules

Assignment rules help you effectively manage customer service workflows by automatically routing incoming chats to different groups. Chats are assigned to team members with the **Customer Agent** role first, or to **Customer Managers** and **Admins** when no **Customer Agents** exist in the group.

When creating or editing a group, you'll see these options in the **Assign rule** section:

Rule| Description  
---|---  
**Round robin (Assign in sequence)**|  Distributes chats evenly across all available agents in the group, rotating through each available agent in sequence to ensure workload balance.  
**Least workload (Assign chat to balance workload)**|  Assigns new chats to the available agent with the fewest current conversations. This assignment rule is ideal for keeping agent workload balanced in real time.  
**Assign to group manager (Assign to manager for manual rerouting)**|  Assigns all incoming chats to designated group managers first, who then manually assign them to the appropriate agent. This assignment rule is ideal for workflows that need senior review initially.   
  
Note that when you create a group with this rule, you're assigned as the group manager by default. To assign this responsibility to a different group manager, first add a customer manager or an admin to the group.  
  
### 

Notification settings

In the **Send email notifications to** section, configure who receives email notifications when chats are assigned to the group:

Option| Description  
---|---  
**Assignee only**|  Sends a notification only to the agent who receives the chat assignment.  
**Group manager and assignee**|  Sends notifications to both the assigned agent and all the group's managers.  
**All agents in the group**|  Sends a notification to every agent in the group.  
  
__Updated about 1 month ago

* * *

# Notify Agent

Customize how your chatbot efficiently transfers conversations to human agents, ensuring timely support and well-informed agents. Leverage the following **Notify agent** settings to streamline customer care with enriched notifications:

  * Agent off-hours settings: Set an automatic reply when messages are sent to your chatbot outside business hours.
  * Pause bot (Notify agent): Temporarily pause automated chatbot responses when a user is talking to a live customer service agent.
  * Notification: Notify customer service agents via LINE notification or email.

![](https://files.readme.io/2855980e929c15bd83e9eef42ba77d0151a97e47daf07f0bcaeee80840e6c9f0-settings-notify-agent.png)

* * *

## 

Agent off-hours settings

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Enable **Agent off-hours settings** by first setting up your [business hours](doc:business-hours).

When this setting is enabled:

  * Customers receive an automated reply if they message outside off-hours.
  * LINE notifications are paused during off-hours, but agents still receive email notifications.
  * Conversations are categorized under **Follow up** , so agents can respond on the next business day.



To handle customer messages outside business hours:

  1. Expand the **Agent off-hours settings** section and turn on the switch.
  2. Click **\+ Reply** to configure [messages](doc:modules-and-messages#messages) and [**User input**](doc:user-input) for the autoreply, then click **Save**.
  3. Select a destination folder for off-hours chat inquiries from the dropdown.

![](https://files.readme.io/07ee5059c325776a1a2dc652422b3a28e5118a07a6378477f3b30086d8d93491-agent-off-hours.png)

* * *

## 

Pause bot (Notify agent)

Pause bot responses while a live agent is assisting a user.

When the [Notify agent](doc:triggered-actions#notify-agent) action is triggered, you can configure how long to pause bot automated responses:

  * Until the close of business (resumes at the end of business hours or when an agent moves the message to Done).
  * For a specified duration in minutes (default is 30 minutes).

![](https://files.readme.io/ed4c430b481cc621404101e967b1de1298316d2de38c289e5a1a436055f27408-pause-bot.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> To learn more about pausing a chatbot for a live chat, see [Pause bot (Live chat)](doc:business-hours#pause-bot-in-live-chat).

* * *

## 

Notification

To effectively notify agents and provide full customer context, set up your preferred notification methods to alert a customer service agents or an [agent group](doc:settings-agent-groups) when the **Notify agent** action is triggered. Notifications can be sent via LINE or email.

Both notification methods will include the user's original message, display name, channel, and connection name to ensure agents have the necessary context from the start.

  1. In your chatbot flows, add a triggered action at **User input** , then select **Notify agent** from the dropdown menu.
  2. Choose the desired agent or agent group to notify.

![](https://files.readme.io/f54a535e57ca0a100255e14633c19ae40e7fc29a1c1a536b7516b53096784468-user-input-notify-agent.png)

### 

Notify via LINE Notification

Set up LINE notifications to alert customer service agents when the **Notify agent** action is triggered. This ensures agents receive real-time updates about customer inquiries, even when they're not actively monitoring the conversation.

![](https://files.readme.io/7847dc3ff4873dd2f30d1c4f9bd09240901ab626def71d3dc9203bb1b9897f3c-LINE_notification.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Each bot can connect to only one LINE OA, which cannot be shared with chatbot services.
>   * Before proceeding, make sure:
>     * The LINE OA is added to the target group.
>     * Your LINE OA has enough message quota to cover notifications (for example, sending to a group of 20 members consumes 20 message credits).
> 


#### 

Connect a LINE Official Account (OA)

  1. Click **\+ Connect LINE OA** , then follow the instructions in LINE Channel Settings to link your LINE OA with a Messaging API channel.

![](https://files.readme.io/66dce82da24d6a362be00e570f76d34995e758cd1c3de80a4515c26201b3e09a-connect-line-oa.png)

  2. After you connect a LINE OA, then click **\+ LINE Notification** on the right. Sign in to your LINE account and select a group or an individual account. A message will be sent once your LINE OA has successfully joined the group.

![](https://files.readme.io/c2e547d3526de0059bd082daabcabdc5cd4a32d06775c3e24c9b4833648e4f6a-line-notification-add-to_group.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The connected LINE OA sends notifications. Make sure the LINE OA is included in the group; otherwise, you won’t receive the "Linking completed" message, and it won’t appear in the notification recipients list.

  3. In the **Agent** column, click the dropdown and select and assign an agent or a group to receive notifications.

![](https://files.readme.io/d4b42bdaf4c53d77e091f8ad6caed907711cd5f886970189a09782f43183758f-assign-agent.png)

  4. Add a trigger action in your [chatbot flows](doc:triggered-actions#chatbot-flows-buttons-quick-replies-and-user-input) by selecting **Notify agent** and customer service agent groups or name from the dropdown.



#### 

Updating your LINE OA and notification recipients

![](https://files.readme.io/98d68618533eb99bc7e4f99c17598657c3ff0ed7bd20ed5bc545df6e888ffed4-update-line-oa.png)

  * Click **\+ LINE Notification** to add another recipient.
  * To use a different LINE OA, click **Update LINE OA** , then delete the existing one and link a new account.
  * Click the trash bin icon on the right side to remove a LINE notification recipient. Ensure the chatbot flow remains linked to a valid group or individual to maintain a valid connection.



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> When deleting or changing the LINE OA:
> 
>   * Existing recipients may be affected, and notifications may not be delivered until they are reassigned.
>   * Check for attention icons on the affected recipients and update the chatbot flow as needed. Make sure the new LINE OA is linked to the same group or individual as before to maintain a valid flow.
> 
![](https://files.readme.io/62a7ba7cf6a64343208e861efcb8198eb4d328c8826b9e209365b2ef7ee2711d-alert-icon.png)

### 

Notify via email

Set up email notifications to alert customer service agents when the [**Notify agent**](doc:triggered-actions#notify-agent) action is triggered. This ensures agents receive updates that help them quickly understand the context and urgency of the inquiry, as shown in the example email below:

  1. User's display name: The name of the user sending the message, for example, Alice.
  2. Channel: The platform where the message originated, such as Facebook or LINE.
  3. Connection name: The specific connection or bot's name, for example, "Appier Shop".
  4. Original message: The content of the user's message, for example, "Need help from agent!".

![](https://files.readme.io/f09a8c70b5b081ebd7e18d83458b97c56bb70b9596bd8794cdae80d01e774694-email-notification-1.png)

To create an email notification group, go to the **Via Email** tab, then click **\+ Email notification** , enter a title, and add email addresses to assign them to a customer service agent or agent group.

![](https://files.readme.io/e295426730a03680d415f65d1c5a6f98f723b5a3bdd04e8097bdc309df8ce8c9-Screen_Recording_2025-07-08_at_3.38.13_PM.gif)

To manage email group notifications:

  * Click the pencil icon to modify the notification recipient.
  * Click the trash bin icon to remove the notification recipient.

# Team Members

You can invite your colleagues to access projects in the Appier console. There are six types of roles:

  * **Admin:** Admins have full access to all project-level settings, including team and agent groups management, billing, and channel integrations.
  * **Editor:** Editors have full permissions to create and manage all chatbot content, except team management and integrations.
  * **Analyst:** Analysts can view chatbot flows and all analytics reports.
  * **Tester:** Testers can view and interact with chatbot flows for testing purposes, but cannot make any changes.
  * **Customer Manager:** Customer managers can manage and assign customer chats, and oversee their own agent groups.
  * **Customer Agent:** Customer agents can view and respond to customer chats that are assigned to them.



To see the detailed permissions granted to each role, refer to the Team member roles and permissions.

* * *

## 

Invite team members

To invite team members, you need to have admin access to the project. Follow the steps below to invite.

  1. From the left menu, go to **Settings** , then select the **Team members** tab, and click **\+ Invite**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e1ba01a9982a92701d54d71d8ab94e91211288199c29d5d346a2538a7650ed4d-team-members.png)

  2. Enter the email addresses, select a role, and click **Send invite**. The invited team members will receive an invitation email with an activation link.
     * You can add multiple email addresses at a time with the same role.
     * If you have multiple projects, you have to send invitations from each project to provide access to each.

![](https://files.readme.io/5c2a16e-enter-email.png)

* * *

## 

Team member roles and permissions

See the following table for the access permissions of each type of role.

### 

Project settings

Permissions| Admin| Editor| Analyst| Tester| Customer manager| Customer agent  
---|---|---|---|---|---|---  
Manage team members| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View and edit billing information| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View, connect, and disconnect channels| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View and edit agent notification settings| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View channel status check| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View API token| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View basic settings| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅  
Edit basic settings| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
  
### 

Chatbot contents and reports

Permissions| Admin| Editor| Analyst| Tester| Customer manager| Customer agent  
---|---|---|---|---|---|---  
View flows| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅  
Edit flows| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
Test bot| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅| ✅  
Deploy bot| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View analytics reports| ✅| ✅| ✅| ❌| ✅| ❌  
View and edit broadcasts| ✅| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Flows includes bot flows, contents, and growth tools.

### 

Customer services

In this system, admins have full control, customer managers act as team leads for their assigned agent groups, and customer agents are the frontline responders who handle assigned chats. For a detailed breakdown of each permission, refer to the tables below.

  * User management
  * Manage chats
  * Manage agent groups
  * Agent group membership



#### 

User management

Permissions| Admin| Editor| Analyst| Tester| Customer manager| Customer agent  
---|---|---|---|---|---|---  
View user information| ✅| ✅| ✅| ❌| ✅| ✅  
Manage bot status for users| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ✅| ✅  
  
#### 

Manage chats

Permissions| Admin| Editor| Analyst| Tester| Customer manager| Customer agent  
---|---|---|---|---|---|---  
View and reply to chats from other agent groups| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
View and reply to chats from own agent group| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ✅| ❌  
View and reply to unassigned chats| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ✅| ✅  
View and reply to chats assigned to them| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ✅| ✅  
Assign chats to members in other agent groups| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
Assign chats to other agent groups following [assignment rules](doc:settings-agent-groups#assignment-rules)| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅| ❌  
Assign chats to anyone in their own group| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ✅| ❌  
Assign chats to another members in the same agent group| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅  
Assign ungrouped chats to any agent| ✅| ✅| ❌| ❌| ✅| ❌  
Assign ungrouped and unassigned chats to themselves| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅  
  
#### 

Manage agent groups

Permissions| Admin| Editor| Analyst| Tester| Customer manager| Customer agent  
---|---|---|---|---|---|---  
Manage all agent groups| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
Manage only own agent group| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅| ❌  
View all agent groups| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅| ❌  
View only own agent group| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅| ✅  
  
#### 

Agent group membership

Permissions| Admin| Editor| Analyst| Tester| Customer manager| Customer agent  
---|---|---|---|---|---|---  
Can be invited to all agent groups| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅| ✅  
Add or remove members in all agent groups| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌| ❌  
Add or remove members in own group| ✅| ❌| ❌| ❌| ✅| ❌  
  
 __Updated 4 months ago

# Split Node

There are three types of nodes that can split users into different paths in the journey:

  * **Check condition**
  * **Check reachability** (only available for BotBonnie channels)
  * **Experiment**

![](https://files.readme.io/6632dd411588f46e5bcc7de35cb2a244ea99d4551512a302a6867720ad9e5b37-split-node-types.png)

* * *

## 

Check condition

Use **Check condition** node to create scenarios to split users into different paths based on their existing events and attributes.

  * If no scenarios are matched, the users will enter the "Not matched" path.
  * The **Check condition** node may not include events from the past hour in its evaluation.



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Split users based on future events
> 
> To split users based on future events, use the **Wait for condition** node instead—the **Check condition** node only checks existing events and attributes.

![](https://files.readme.io/dcfe33f-condition_path.png)

If you create multiple scenarios, the ones closer to the top of the list have higher priority. Users who meet the conditions of multiple scenarios will enter the path of the scenario closer to the top. You can adjust the order of the scenarios by clicking the arrow buttons.

![](https://files.readme.io/4bd4fea-Check_condition.png)

* * *

## 

Check reachability

You can split users based on their reachability for the following channels:

  * LINE
  * Facebook Messenger
  * WebChat
  * WhatsApp



Select the channel next to **Check if the users are reachable on** and select the LINE Official Account. If needed, click **Add Channel** to add more paths for other channels.

![](https://files.readme.io/20b8779-reachable.png)   


* * *

## 

Experiment

Randomly split users into different paths conduct A/B testing, allowing you to identify the most effective journey paths.

  1. (Optional) Check **Enable control group** to create a control group. Users in the control group will be immediately be redirected to the end of the journey.
  2. Click **\+ Add test path** to add alternate journey paths. You can add up to five paths.
  3. Under **Traffic allocation** , enter the percentage of users that should be sent to each path or click **Divide equally** to balance traffic equally between all paths, including the control group. The total percentage must add up to 100%.

![](https://files.readme.io/ead8e8aef22974e91058b84fa3d0e3412d26dedc61daaf902907739432790173-create-experiment-node.gif)

__Updated 7 months ago

* * *

# Status Check

The status check page allows you to view:

  * Bot usage (by feature): View quotas and quota usage for your bot.
  * The status of your connected channels: See if your messaging channels are connected properly, and fix connection settings if they aren't.



To open the status page, go to **Settings** , then click the **Status check** tab.

![](https://files.readme.io/265b629-nav-status-check.png)

* * *

## 

Bot usage

Click the arrow to expand the **Bot usage** section. Each feature will have a colored dot representing its current status, and if there is a quota, it will be displayed on the right side.

Status color| Description  
---|---  
Green| There is remaining quota or there is no usage limit for this feature.  
Yellow| There is no remaining quota for this feature.  
Red| This feature was previously active, but is currently inactive.  
Gray| This feature has never been used or is not enabled.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/ff2957a-bot-usage.png)

* * *

## 

Channel status

Click the arrow on the right to expand the **Channel status** section. Each channel will have a colored dot representing its current status.

Status color| Description  
---|---  
Green| No connection issues detected.  
Yellow| Issues detected in the connected platform (e.g. LINE, Instagram) that may affect some functions of your bot.  
Red| Connection issues detected.  
  
If a channel has connection issues, a button will appear on the right side, allowing you to initiate the connection repairing process.

![](https://files.readme.io/6a7d189-channel-status.png)

__Updated 5 months ago

# Survey

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
> The survey kit is available for the following channels:
> 
>   * Facebook
>   * Instagram
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
>   * WhatsApp
>   * Kakao Talk
> 


The survey kit allows you to conduct surveys or have users fill out forms in a more interactive way. Using the survey kit, you can easily:

  * Collect and save survey responses in a Google spreadsheet.
  * Store users' answers as profile information, user attributes, or user tags.
  * Reward users who successfully completed the survey.



### 

Example use cases

Here are some examples of how the survey kit can be used:

  * Generate leads by having users fill out contact information for an event you're hosting.
  * Conduct a customer satisfaction survey to collect feedback and segment users based on their satisfaction level.
  * Collect product surveys and save users' preferences as tags for precise targeting.

![](https://files.readme.io/dec5c1304f6d9fc32b80e368df3878264c91aeff67c225eb98127890294162a5-chat-screenshot.png)

* * *

## 

Survey kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

Go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Survey**. You can either start from a template or a blank survey kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/5249f3082641ccd631d52f522a20edd9235ed7ca9c2f1e1ffa59f1de900f2ed5-add-survey-kit.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to enter the sub-flow of the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/5b94e7c-survey-kit-start-editing.png)

Click the **Start** circle.

![](https://files.readme.io/6c92607726aed595f54bf0f5305ec82e5e1504945597448d2d6b87d15ec16a22-start-survey-kit.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic kit settings

Under the **Basic** section, complete the following settings.

  * **Name** : Enter a kit name that helps you identify the kit on the console.
  * **Campaign period** : To set a campaign period, switch on the toggle and set the start time and the end time. Select an **Out-of-period module** to display if the user enters the kit outside the campaign period.
  * **Survey responses** : Enter your Google Mail account. The survey responses will be stored as a Google spreadsheet in this Google account.
    * **Change owner** : To change the owner of the sheet, type a different Google account and click **Change owner**. The existing survey responses will be transferred to the new account.
    * **Open spreadsheet** : Click to see the survey results collected.
  * **Start module** : Select the module to display to the users when they start the campaign.
  * **End module** : Select the ending module to display to the users when they finish the survey.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If the user leaves the survey halfway through and doesn't reach the final end module, the survey responses already provided by the user will not be recorded in the Google spreadsheet.

![](https://files.readme.io/d55e6acdb187196bcd6d8eac41212e834acd9581d91c434436490320e4a2cc7d-surveykit-settings.png)

### 

3\. Set the survey responses

Under **Response columns** settings, you'll see two types of user data you can collect in the survey response:

  * **Auto-recorded info** : User information that's automatically collected by the messaging platforms, such as LINE and Facebook Messenger. The user information in the **Answer type** column will be labeled in blue.
  * **User-submitted info** : Each question you add as a module in your flow automatically creates a corresponding response column in the spreadsheet. The **Answer type** column will store the user's answer to that specific question and labeled in gray.



Use the following settings to adjust how you want the Google spreadsheet to look like.

  * Switch on the **Save** toggle for any user information you want to store in the spreadsheet.
  * You can edit the **Column name** of the user-submitted info in the spreadsheet.
  * You can adjust the order of the columns displayed in the spreadsheet by dragging the reposition icon next to each column to reorder it.

![](https://files.readme.io/3ee8f0a-Screen_Shot_2023-07-03_at_10.27.54_AM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * If you change the column order after responses are recorded, existing data in your Google spreadsheet won't reorder to match the new arrangement.
>   * Previously collected information might become misaligned with your new columns.
> 

> 
> To avoid this, finalize your column order before collecting live survey responses.

### 

4\. Configure survey questions in your flow

In the default template of the survey kit flow, your survey questions are the modules between the start module and the end module.

![](https://files.readme.io/fb8e3b7b06df06283848cbd4ef99c61e15d674f04cdf497041dac54c510e62ea-survey-questions.png)

  * Click **Add module** to add a new question, or click an existing module in the default template to edit.
  * You can encourage users to participate in the survey by providing a reward. For example, you can let users know about the reward in the **Start** module and connect the **Done** module to a lucky wheel or scratch-off kit.



#### 

Add a multiple-choice question

To set up a multiple-choice question, click **Add quick reply** to add your answer options. Users who click on different answers can be directed to a different module.

![](https://files.readme.io/a777d6b-Screen_Shot_2023-06-30_at_10.37.38_AM.png)

#### 

Add an input validation

To add an input validation, click the **User input** tab in the module, click **Add behavior** , select **Input format** , and select one of the input formats.

  * **Phone number** : The user input has to be a number between four and ten digits, without any symbols.
  * **Email** : The user input has to be in email format with an @ symbol followed by a domain name.
  * **Date** : The user input has to be one of the following formats: `yyyy-mm-dd`, `yyyy/mm/dd`, `yyyy.mm.dd`, `yyyymmdd`, `mm-dd-yyyy`, `mm/dd/yyyy`, `mm.dd.yyyy`, `mmddyyyy`
    * Day-month-year format is not accepted (for example, 30/9/2023).
    * The date must include the year, month, and day. A date with just the month and year is not accepted (for example, 9/2023).
    * If the format includes slashes, hyphens, or periods, the month and day can be a single digit without a 0 in front (for example, 9/1/2023). For `mmddyyyy` and `yyyymmdd`, you must add a `0` before a single-digit day or month and make sure there are eight digits total. For example, "May 8, 2023" must be written as `05082023` or `20230508`.
  * **Number** : The user input has to be a number.

![](https://files.readme.io/805b498-Screen_Shot_2023-06-30_at_10.11.42_AM.png)

When the user enters the correct input format, the **Bot reply** should go to the next question module or the end module. When the user enters incorrect input formats, the **Bot reply** should go to a module that reminds users to use the correct format.

![](https://files.readme.io/f3796d7-Screen_Shot_2023-06-30_at_10.27.51_AM.png)

#### 

Store survey answers as user attributes

Answers submitted by users will be automatically stored in a Google spreadsheet, but you can also store them as user profile information, user tags, or user parameters for future marketing purposes. For example, if your user answered that their favorite ice cream flavor is "vanilla", you can store this as a user tag.

![](https://files.readme.io/515848b-Screen_Shot_2023-06-30_at_11.07.48_AM.png)

To do this, click the **User input** tab in the question module and under **Triggered actions** , click **Set up triggered actions** under the correct input behavior. Click **Add action** and select one of the options below:

  * **Add tag**
  * **Save as user demographic** (Phone, email, birthday, location, gender)
  * **Save user parameter**



For configuration details, see [Triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions#triggered-action-settings).

![](https://files.readme.io/b38b434-survey-kit-triggered-action.png)

### 

5\. Check the survey responses

To see the survey responses, simply open the Google spreadsheet. Go to **Survey kit settings** and click **Open spreadsheet**. You need to be logged into the Google account.

![](https://files.readme.io/6afa4c3-Screen_Shot_2023-06-30_at_11.29.37_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/6a5ed50-survey_response.png)

__Updated 5 months ago

# Test and Publish the Journey

## 

Test the journey

After you've finished creating your journey, you can test the journey to visualize how a user traverses the journey before publishing it. From the top bar, click **Test journey**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6354f0a-Screen_Shot_2024-03-22_at_10.52.10_AM.png)

To add a test user, click **Select user** and search for the test user. Next, select a user, and click **Select**.

  * Search by entering the complete email, phone number, or `user_id`. The search results only include users who exactly match the data you entered. Searches are case-sensitive.
  * You can only add one test user.

![](https://files.readme.io/b5247db-Screen_Shot_2024-03-25_at_10.12.48_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/9051661-Screen_Shot_2024-03-25_at_10.10.41_AM.png)

Under **Wait time settings** , you can shorten the wait nodes to speed up the test process. Select **Set all wait times (time delay nodes, time-out settings) to** and set a new wait time. The wait time will be applied to the following nodes.

  * Time delay nodes
  * Wait for conditions nodes: Time-out settings
  * Message nodes: Time-out settings

![](https://files.readme.io/5645ba8-Screen_Shot_2024-03-25_at_10.15.25_AM.png)

Under **Trigger node settings** , if you select **Bypass the trigger node** , the test user will automatically enter the journey even if they do not meet the trigger condition.

![](https://files.readme.io/2156b61-Screen_Shot_2024-03-25_at_10.15.25_AM_copy.png)

Finally, click **Start test journey** to begin testing. In test mode, you can see the test user's position in the journey.

![](https://files.readme.io/a803e0a-Test_user_journey.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> You may be subject to messaging fees when you send email and SMS messages during the test.

* * *

## 

Publish the journey

When you have completed the settings, click **Publish**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a38267e-Screen_Shot_2023-12-05_at_12.20.19_PM.png)

Once the journey starts running, its status will change to **Active**.

![](https://files.readme.io/20ad438-Screen_Shot_2023-12-05_at_12.14.33_PM.png)

### 

Editing the journey after publishing

To make edits after publishing, you need to pause the journey first. To pause a journey, click the three dots next to the journey's name, then click **Pause journey map**.

![](https://files.readme.io/85c3f99-pause-journey.png)

Please note that making certain types of edits on a journey after publishing it may affect performance data.

  * Changing the journey structure (e.g. moving a node to another position) will clear the performance data of each affected node.
  * Modifying a creative in a message node has no effect on that node's performance data.



__Updated 11 months ago

* * *

[Journey Schedule and Re-entry Settings](/docs/journey-schedule-and-re-entry-settings)[Journey Performance and Analytics](/docs/journey-performance-and-analytics)

# Triggered Actions

## 

Overview

Triggered actions allow you to automate tasks and customize user interactions in your chatbot. You can add or remove user tags, save user parameters, and perform other actions at specific trigger points during a conversation. For a comprehensive list of available actions and detailed configuration instructions, see the Triggered action settings section.

### 

Trigger points

The following trigger points can be used to complete a triggered action:

  * [Buttons](doc:button) (Postback module, Open URL, Share on Facebook, Share with LINE Friend, Open live agent chatroom)
  * [Quick replies](doc:quick-reply)
  * [User input](doc:user-input)
  * Starting a conversation by URL or QR code (Growth tools)
  * Conditions kits in advanced kits or rules kits
  * Menu groups (Supported for Messenger and LINE)



* * *

## 

Adding a triggered action

Triggered actions can be added at specific trigger points:

  * Chatbot flows: Buttons, quick replies, and user input
  * Growth tools: Start a chat with short URL or QR code



### 

Chatbot flows: Buttons, quick replies, and user input

Triggered actions can be executed in chatbot flows when:

  * A user clicks a [button](doc:button)
  * A user responds with a [quick reply](doc:quick-reply)
  * A [user input](doc:user-input) rule is met



To set up triggered actions in your flow:

  1. Go to the flows page and open the module you'd like to edit.
  2. Add a button, quick reply, or user input behavior condition.



Trigger point| Instructions  
---|---  
[Button](doc:button)| ![](https://files.readme.io/2dda1c8-triggered-action-add-button.png)  
[Quick reply](doc:quick-reply)| ![](https://files.readme.io/5c44241-triggered-action-add-quick-reply.png)  
[User input](doc:user-input) (**Standard chat**)| ![](https://files.readme.io/1c9c64d-user-input-behavior-condition.png)  
  
  3. Under **Triggered actions** , click **Set up triggered actions**. Click **\+ Add triggered action** and select an action from the dropdown.

![](https://files.readme.io/3aa8222-triggered-actions-user-input.png)

  4. Configure the required settings for the selected action. See Triggered action settings for details.



### 

Growth tools: Start a chat with short URL or QR code

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported platforms
> 
> The following platforms support triggered actions in growth tools:
> 
>   * LINE
>   * Facebook
>   * Instagram
> 


  1. Go to **Growth tools** and click **\+ Add growth tool**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6fe6cc0-triggered-action-add-growth-tool.png)

  2. Select **Start chat with short URL** or **Start chat with QR code**.

![](https://files.readme.io/314913b-triggered-action-growth-tool-type.png)

  3. Under **Triggered actions** , click **Set up triggered actions**. Click **\+ Add triggered action** and select an action from the dropdown.

![](https://files.readme.io/9b26180-triggered-actions-growth-tools.png)

  4. Configure the required settings for the selected action. See Triggered action settings for details.



* * *

## 

Triggered action settings

Refer to the sections below to learn more about the required settings and configuration for each triggered action type.

  * User profile management:
    * Add/remove tag: Modify user profile tags.
    * Save as user demographic: Capture and store user-specific information.
    * Save/Remove parameter: Create, update, or remove custom user parameters.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> You can view user tags, demographics, and parameters in these sections:
> 
>   * [Live chat](doc:live-chat#managing-user-details): User profile on the right side of **Live chat**.
>   * [User list](doc:user-list#detailed-user-view): Detailed user view in **Audience** > **User list**.
>   * [User properties](doc:user-properties): View the parameters and user demographics in **Audience** > **User properties**.
> 


  * Communication and routing:
    * Notify agent: Notify a customer service agent.
    * Assign to Messenger menu group: Assign the user to a Messenger menu group.
    * Assign to LINE menu group: Assign the user to a LINE menu group.
    * Send conversion event to Meta: Send and track the conversion event data in Messenger.
  * Data and integration:
    * Calculate parameter value: Perform mathematical operations on user parameters
    * HTTP request: Send a **`GET`** or **`POST`** HTTP request to the specified URL.
    * Point card: Display a point card from a supported advanced kit.



### 

Add tag

Select from the dropdown to choose an existing tag or type in a new tag to add to the user's profile.

![](https://files.readme.io/a41cce1-triggered-actions-add-tag.png)

### 

Remove tag

Select from the dropdown to choose the tags you'd like to remove from the user's profile.

![](https://files.readme.io/e99fb32-triggered-actions-remove-tag.png)

### 

Notify agent

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Before using the **Notify agent** trigger action, ensure notification settings are configured in **Settings >[Notify agent](doc:notify-agent)**.
> 
> When triggered by **User input** in your chatbot flow, the [notification](doc:notify-agent#notification) (via LINE or email) will include the user's original message, display name, channel, and connection name.

From the dropdown, select the customer service agents which should be notified. After the **Notify agent** action is triggered:

  1. The user's conversation will be moved to the **Main** folder of the live chat menu.
  2. By default, the chatbot pauses for 30 minutes. To modify the duration, go to **Settings** > **[Pause bot (Notify agent)](doc:notify-agent#pause-bot-notify-agent)**.

![](https://files.readme.io/325b92e-triggered-actions-notify-agent.png)

### 

Save as user demographic

From the dropdown, select the user demographic attribute to save. The value of the demographic attribute can come from the following sources:

  * the user's last message
  * a button label value
  * a quick reply value



For example, if you prompted the user to input their email address in the previous message, select **User email** from the dropdown.

![](https://files.readme.io/5df9460-triggered-actions-save-as-user-demographic.png)

### 

Save user parameter

From the first dropdown, select one or more existing parameters or create new parameter to save to the user's profile.

Next, click on each parameter you added to finalize its settings. Choose whether the parameter should be set by user input (**User Input**) or if you'd like to determine the value (**Parameter value**).

  * **User Input** : The value of the parameter will be set to the last message the user sent. If this action isn't triggered by user input, e.g. triggered via button click, no value is saved.
  * **Parameter value** : Input the value of the parameter.

![](https://files.readme.io/337e27f-triggered-actions-save-user-parameter.png)

### 

Calculate parameter value

  1. Under **Calculate parameter value** , build your parameter calculation formula.
     1. To insert an existing user parameter into the formula, click **Add parameter**.
     2. To insert mathematical operators, click the buttons in the toolbar. Consecutive operators are not allowed, e.g. `1++2`, `5+-1`, `3*/3`.
     3. To insert numbers, type them directly into the input box. Special characters can't be manually entered—use the toolbar to use mathematical operators instead.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Division by 0 will result in an invalid null value.
>   * Results are rounded down to the closest integer value.
> 


![](https://files.readme.io/5118170-triggered-action-calculate-parameter-value-formula.png)

  2. Under **Save result to a parameter** , select or create the parameter to save the formula's result into. You can select an existing parameter from the dropdown or create a new parameter by entering a name and clicking **Create:**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a3fef7b-triggered-action-calculate-parameter-value-save-result.png)

### 

Remove parameter

From the dropdown, select one or more parameters to remove from the user's profile.

![](https://files.readme.io/395e26f-triggered-actions-remove-parameter.png)

### 

HTTP request

From the dropdown, select the HTTP method (**`GET`** or **`POST`**), then enter the URL to send the request to.

When this action is triggered, a request containing bot details will be sent to the URL you specified. An example request looks like this:

JSON
    
    
    {
      "bot_id": "bot-cS7svydXc312",
      "bot_raw_uid": "Ua96ed50ebbdef329328507c1",
      "bot_uid": "a71f2cedd1359d39a3d72ad9e04a2b0ebddcc4e1ec3af284de945",
      "bot_pid": "859djl",
      "bot_channel": 1,
      "env": "prod"
    }

### 

Assign to Messenger menu group

From the dropdown, select the Messenger menu group to assign the user to. Menu groups can be managed in **Content > Menus**.

![](https://files.readme.io/be36d7b-triggered-actions-assign-to-messenger-menu-group.png)

### 

Assign to LINE menu group

From the dropdown, select the LINE menu group to assign the user to. Menu groups can be managed in **Content > Menus**.

![](https://files.readme.io/eba5cfc-triggered-actions-assign-to-line-menu-group.png)

### 

Send conversion event to Meta

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> [Enable the Meta Conversion API integration](doc:connecting-facebook-account-to-botbonnie#enable-the-meta-conversion-api-integration) to send a conversion event to the Meta Business Suite.

From the dropdown, select a conversion event to send the data. The event data will be visible in the Meta Business Suite console for tracking key customer actions in Messenger:

  * **Add to cart**
  * **Purchase**
  * **Submit lead**
  * **Initiate checkout**

![](https://files.readme.io/55606243683ce06952aaf1f985534bf5e3f7116570f01fc0053dee1c524cb81c-send-conversion-event-to-meta.png)

### 

Point card

You can display an existing point card and issue points from one of the following advanced kits:

  * MGM (member-get-member)
  * Serial number verification
  * Receipt registration



For details on creating and triggering a point card, see [Point Card Kit](doc:point-card-kit).

![](https://files.readme.io/dd5da11-Frame_1.png)

__Updated 8 months ago

* * *

# Update Users Node

## 

Overview

Use the update users node allows you to modify a user's details when they enter the node.

![](https://files.readme.io/1cb2e7a-add-update-users-node.png)

* * *

## 

Update user attributes

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> This node type is only supported for users on BotBonnie channels. It has no effect for users on AIQUA channels.

Click on the node to open its settings.

  * To configure tags to add to the user, click the input box under **Add the following tags to users**.
  * To configure tags to remove from the user, click the input box under **Remove the following tags to users**

![](https://files.readme.io/98b0519-update-user-attribute-add-remove-tags.png)

To add an existing tag, select from the list of tags that appear, then click **Ok**.

  
![](https://files.readme.io/02a7f3a-update-user-attribute-existing-tag.png)

To add a new tag, type the tag name directly into the input box, then click **Create tag**.

![](https://files.readme.io/b0d8272-update-user-attribute-new-tag.png)

__Updated 11 months ago

# User Input

You can configure individual modules to handle specific user inputs. Based on keywords the user enters, your chatbot can respond with a targeted module or run [triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions).

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> How user input works
> 
> User input settings apply only to the module where you configure them. If the user's input doesn't match any specific conditions, the bot checks the rule-based replies and default replies configured in [Automation](doc:automation).

* * *

## 

Set up user input

  1. Click the module to open the edit screen, then go to the **User input** tab. Under **User input method** , choose **Standard chat**.

![](https://files.readme.io/030454d8adf87c781f5a311ced16ab52cbc44c35aba125acec82aa2204b1ee34-user-input.png)

  2. Click **\+ Add behavior**. Under **When user inputs** , configure the criteria for the rule.
     1. You can set rules based on the input content, type, or format (such as a phone number or email address).
     2. To add more conditions, click **\+ Add rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f8e1b613bff376ae94f32c7733fda8586aa497c8d72f519fd26692e01d115782-user-input-behavior.png)

  3. Click the **Bot reply** dropdown and select the module that should be sent when the input meets the conditions.

![](https://files.readme.io/23066644e33a76cf13c13e9a52a3cc2c3dab32dc8cc0ccb7b37a45e2b27fbebb-user-input-bot-reply.png)

  3. (Optional) Under **Triggered actions** , click **Set up triggered actions** to execute a triggered action when the input meets the conditions. For configuration details, see [Triggered Actions](doc:triggered-actions).

![](https://files.readme.io/c4c2574f55fe5730894d8101cde1dfdc09f771cd1578cbf3e790f79568364145-user-input-triggered-actions.png)

__Updated about 1 month ago

* * *

# User List

BotBonnie's audience list feature consolidates users from all your connected social platforms, such as Instagram and LINE Official Accounts, into one console. It offers advanced filtering options, including user tags, interaction time, and account linking status. Marketers and customer service agents can easily:

  * View user profiles
  * Add and remove tags
  * Manage subscriptions
  * Assign users to a menu group



The feature also supports bulk actions like setting LINE menu groups, exporting data, and importing users, making it a powerful tool for efficient audience management and personalized customer interactions.

* * *

## 

User list

If the chatbot is connected to supported channels, such as an Instagram account or LINE Official Account, all users from every platform can be seen simultaneously on the same console. In the audience list, you can see the following details for all users:

  * Profile picture
  * Name
  * First interaction time
  * Last interaction time
  * Platform
  * Channel
  * Subscription status
  * Bot status



To view the user list, go to **Audience > User list**.

![](https://files.readme.io/97bd8b5-user-list.png)

### 

Detailed user view

To open the detailed user view, click the eye icon on the right side of the user list. In this view, you can see a user's specific details and modify their tags.

![](https://files.readme.io/6081029-user-list-user-details.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/e3924da-user-details.png)

### 

Filter the user list

You can also filter out lists of users with specific information you want to see through filtering. To use multiple filters simultaneously, click **\+ Add condition**. When using multiple conditions, you can choose how they work together:

  * **Match all** : Target users who meet all rules you set.
  * **Match any** : Target users who meet at least one rule you set.

![](https://files.readme.io/5121fa7-user-list-filter-conditions.png)

#### 

Available filter conditions

The following table describes all available filter conditions and how they work:

Filter condition| Description  
---|---  
**Tag**|  Target users based on their assigned tags. You can use different operators:  
  
• **Has any of** : Users with at least one selected tag  
• **Has all of** : Users with all selected tags  
• **Doesn 't have any of**: Users without any selected tags  
• **Doesn 't have all of**: Users missing at least one selected tag  
**Tag confidence index**|  Target users based on how strongly a tag applies to them. Tag confidence scores range from 1 to 10 and are calculated using the user's last tagged date and tag count.  
  
• Scores increase when users perform relevant actions. Higher scores indicate users who most likely match the tag characteristics  
• Scores decrease over time when users don't interact with tag-related content  
**Tag count**|  Filter users based on how many times they've been tagged with a specific tag. For example, find users tagged as `frequent_buyer` more than three times.  
**Tagged date**|  Filter users based on when they were tagged. Useful for targeting users from specific campaigns or time periods.  
**First interaction**|  Filter users based on when they first interacted with your bot. Helpful for identifying new users versus long-time users.  
**Last interaction**|  Filter users based on their most recent interaction. Use this to find active or inactive users.  
**Account linking status**|  Filter users by whether their account is linked to your system (**Linked** or **Not linked**).  
**Menu Group**|  Filter users based on their assigned LINE or Messenger menu group.  
**One-time notification list**|  Find users who have opted in for [one-time notifications](doc:message-type-one-time-notification-request).  
**Recurring notifications**|  Find users who have opted in for [recurring notifications](doc:message-type-recurring-notifications-request).  
**Platform**|  Filter users by social platform, such as LINE, Instagram, and Messenger.  
**Not been sent broadcast recently**|  Find users who haven't received broadcasts in the past 1-60 days to avoid over-messaging.  
**Birthday**|  Filter users based on their birthday month.  
**Parameters**|  Filter users based on custom data fields stored in their profile. For example, filter users where `purchase_count` is greater than 5.  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Using filters in other features
> 
> These same filter conditions are also available when creating [broadcast audiences](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts#3-filter-the-broadcast-audience) and [segments](doc:segments).

* * *

## 

Modifying user details and settings

### 

Enable or disable the bot services

If a user does not need bot services (e.g., when a response from a real customer service representative is required), just turn the status of the user’s bot to “off.” There is no need to cancel the link between the fan page and bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/a1f30af-modify-bot-status.png)

### 

Edit the user's subscription status

You can unsubscribe users directly from the user list by clicking the **Subscribed** button corresponding to the user.

![](https://files.readme.io/61fe9f4-unsubscribe-user.png)

### 

Bulk operations

In addition to modifying an individual user profile from the detailed user view, you can bulk update users from the user list by:

  1. Checking the box next to each user you'd like to update.
  2. Clicking the **Bulk actions** dropdown and selecting the operation you'd like to complete.



The following bulk operations are available:

  * **Add Tag**
  * **Remove tag**
  * **Unsubscribe**
  * **Assign to Messenger**
  * **Assign to LINE menu**
  * **Export user data**
    * [Export User Data for LINE Ads](doc:export-user-data-for-line-ads)
    * [Export User Data for Meta Ads](doc:export-user-data-for-meta-ads)

![](https://files.readme.io/2b20c1c-user-list-bulk-operations.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/2866754-bulk-operation-examples.png)

* * *

## 

Import users

From the user list, you can seamlessly upload user data to BotBonnie. To import users, complete the following steps:

  1. **Select a channel to import** : Select the channel (e.g., Facebook, LINE) for which you want to import user IDs.
  2. **Upload a User ID File** : Upload a CSV file containing user IDs, ensuring it doesn't exceed the size limit of 10 MB. Only user IDs of friends following your LINE Official Account will be processed.
  3. **Tag these users** : Assign relevant tags to these users during the import process to enhance user segmentation and targeting.

![](https://files.readme.io/7bcb488-import-users.png)

### 

When should I import users?

Consider the following scenario:

  1. A user followed the brand account on May 1.
  2. The brand account was connected to BotBonnie on June 1, but the user didn't interact with the brand account after June 1.
  3. Since BotBonnie didn't detect any activity, this user isn't in BotBonnie's database.



Because BotBonnie's database isn't aware of this user, you'll need to import the user manually.

### 

Importing LINE users

  * You can use the [LINE Messaging API](https://developers.line.biz/en/reference/messaging-api/#get-follower-ids) to retrieve the user IDs required for the import file.
  * If your LINE Official Account is certified, you have the option to import users to BotBonnie automatically by selecting **System automatically obtains user ID**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a9e747f-line-auto-sync-en.png)   


__Updated 4 months ago

* * *

# User Properties

User properties help you organize and track information about your users through tags and parameters. Use tags to group users based on shared characteristics, and parameters to store specific user attributes.

> 👍
> 
> You can also view each user's tags and parameters in the [User list](doc:user-list#user-list) and user details in [live chat](doc:live-chat).

![](https://files.readme.io/d3a128df07adf902fa6c6f29f7ff88e3e00a2c564f852b510c9884638ef6d981-user-properties-.png)

* * *

## 

Tags

There are two types of tags you can categorize your users:

  * **User tag** (blue): These are tags you create manually to categorize users based on their characteristics, behaviors, or preferences.
  * **Synced tag** (yellow): These tags are automatically synced from a connected e-commerce platform and are associated with a user's purchased items. [E-commerce integration](doc:e-commerce-integrations) is a beta feature. Contact your customer support manager to learn more.



To view and manage tags, go to **Audience** > **User properties** and select the **Tags** tab.

![](https://files.readme.io/a2c62437495fe9b623f1c1200b7b71072e13ef16ab381abd6615a2e1679f1d0f-user-properties-tag.png)

### 

Tag folders

Use tag folders to organize your tags. Click **\+ Create folder** to add a new folder.

![](https://files.readme.io/01f4bf503479df0853d99aafa43a0f2986a636e2bcc7224f29a0e028954734ff-tags-list-create-folder.png)

### 

Manage individual tag

Each tag displays its name, type, and the number of users associated with it. You can perform the following actions:

  * Add: Click **\+ Create tag** to add a new tag.
  * Move: Click the folder icon or use drag-and-drop to move tags into folders.
  * Edit or delete: Click the pencil icon to rename it, or click the trash bin icon to remove it.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Synced tags (yellow) are managed by e-commerce platform and cannot be edited or deleted here.

![](https://files.readme.io/37f71a1258750724ca60389e2d58bd4d8eab8cf0701eaa748a1813afdca46211-create-tag-tag-list.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> New tags appear under **Uncategorized tags** until you move them into a folder.

* * *

## 

Parameters

Use parameters to store and reference custom user data, including demographic information.

To manage parameters, go to **User properties** > **Parameters**.

![](https://files.readme.io/d54c613f9c523e6432918a2d1f5ac26824d368bd9396f768a2b2b830ff956a54-parameters-new.png)

### 

Custom parameters

Create and manage custom parameters to store user information:

  * Add: Click **\+ New parameter** , then enter a name and select the parameter's data type (text or number).
  * Edit: Click the pencil icon to modify the name or type of the parameter.
  * Delete: Click the trash bin icon on the right of parameter to delete.

![](https://files.readme.io/c8e379d4ccb314df705fa6914587ae5e1a175c840af5fbf2221cf5f76319f53c-parameters-list.png)

### 

User demographics

User demographics are default attributes that can be used to store basic user information such as birthday, gender, phone number, location, and email address.

![](https://files.readme.io/461c949f38dc01c7bb9fe66e17404825825e08fc13275a2d1571ef4b90800299-user-demographics.png)

# Wait Node

Wait nodes allow you to control when users should move to the next node. To add a wait node, click the circle in between nodes and select one of the following types:

  * Time delay
  * Wait for condition

![](https://files.readme.io/6c63730-Wait.png)

* * *

## 

Time delay

You can keep the users at the current node for a period of time or until a time slot.

![](https://files.readme.io/65d937a-wait_time_delay.png)

  * **Wait for a period of time** : Users are moved to the next node in the journey map after a certain period of time has passed. You can set a duration under **How long should people wait for**.
  * **Wait until a time slot** : Users are moved to the next node in the journey map when they reach a specified timeslot during the day or during the week.
    * Select **Daily** if you want to set timeslots during certain hours of the day.
    * Select **Weekly** if you want to set timeslots during certain days of the week.



* * *

## 

Wait for condition

Users will move to the next node in the journey map when they perform certain events.

  * You can select up to three event-based conditions.
  * Users who satisfy any of the event-based conditions will move to the next node.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Event parameters: `is not ` operator
> 
> When setting event parameter conditions, the `is not` operator evaluates to `true` if the event doesn't contain the parameter. For example, `"product_category" is not "book"` will evaluate to `true` if the entry trigger event doesn't contain the `product_category` event parameter.

If the user doesn't complete the event within the timeout period, they'll be moved to the **Timeout** path. Under **Timeout** , you can click the edit icon to set the timeout period.

![](https://files.readme.io/1d198c4-Screen_Shot_2023-10-19_at_12.31.24_AM.png)   


__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

[Split Node](/docs/split-node)[Update Users Node](/docs/update-users-node)
